**The PN stage can be fitted to the dendrites, mostly by a gain per glomerulus.** Every fitted stage is nearer the held-out dendrites than the model's own, and the largest step is to the measured gains, which need no fit. The terms between glomeruli add little. The global stage ends at a bound with the normalization nearly off, which is hard to defend in an antennal lobe known to have that gain control.

The spread of the odors' totals before the calyx with each stage put in, as in [the table above](#before-calyx). The values of the three stages put in are of the model's own PNs and boutons of the shared glomeruli.

| | the flies | the best family | the recorded<br>dendrites imposed | the global stage | the stage with<br>glomerulus terms |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| the PNs (in the flies, the PN dendrites) | {{E90/pn_stage.csv:reading=pn_dend:cv_totals ~ .3f}} | {{E90/pn_stage.csv:reading=change:cv_totals ~ .3f}} | {{E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/writeup_values.csv:arm=imposed;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;statistic=totals_cv_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/writeup_values.csv:arm=R1;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;statistic=totals_cv_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/writeup_values.csv:arm=rung;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;statistic=totals_cv_mean:value ~ .3f}} |
| the PN boutons | {{E119/a2_spread.csv:criterion=2.0:totals_cv_mean ~ .3f}} | {{E120/a1_summary.csv:stage=bouton;n=1.0;form=baseline;reading=rate;criterion=2.0:totals_cv ~ .3f}} | {{E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/writeup_values.csv:arm=imposed;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;statistic=totals_cv_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/writeup_values.csv:arm=R1;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;statistic=totals_cv_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/writeup_values.csv:arm=rung;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;statistic=totals_cv_mean:value ~ .3f}} |

**The evenness before the calyx was not what held the somata back.** Every stage put in spreads the odors' totals far more widely than the family's own, at the PNs most of the way to the flies'. The somata's strengths by odor still move away from the flies'. So the family's calyx turns a less even input into the wrong strengths by odor.

**Limits.**

- **The calyx was not fitted again.** These PN stages were tried on the family's calyx as it stood, and none was handed on to a fit of the calyx. In the best family fitted to all megamat stages at once ([below](#stage-fit)) the model's own normalization is searched together with two scales of the calyx instead. There the dendrites, the boutons and the claws come nearer the flies on wirings and flies that the fit did not see.
- **The recorded dendrites have since been read under a fitted calyx.** Arranged on the fitted model's own PN rates, with its normalization and both of its calyx scales, they bring the dendrites nearer the flies and move the boutons and the claws away. So the verdict on the recorded dendrites does not rest on the family's calyx ([below](#dendrite-bouton-step)).
- **The dendrite recordings hold {{json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/lock.json:len(glomeruli)}} of the model's {{json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/timing_step1.json:size.n_input}} input glomeruli.** The others stay at the model's rates, and the odor that carries most of the family's error at the megamat somata is fed mostly through them ([below](#strength-entries)).

### The odors that carry the strength error and the glomeruli that feed them {#strength-entries}

**The question was what the family's error on strength by odor at the KC somata is made of,** odor by odor on every panel. The experiment is the odors that carry the strength error and the glomeruli that feed them, and it ran no model. A single odor's share of an error is a lead, so every share was read on two halves of the flies as well.

**On megamat the error is aa's.** aa carries {{E138/entries.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common;odor=aa:view_share ~ .2f}} of the squared error of the family as fitted, and {{E138/halves.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=fit;row=common;odor=aa:view_share ~ .2f}} and {{E138/halves.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=held;row=common;odor=aa:view_share ~ .2f}} on the two halves of the flies. Without aa's entry the error would read {{E138/entries.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common;odor=aa:view_error_without ~ .2f}} in place of {{E138/entries.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common;odor=aa:view_error ~ .2f}}, and {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:view_error_without_first ~ .2f}} in place of {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:view_error ~ .2f}} with the family held, against the flies' floor of {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=kc_soma:strength_floor ~ .2f}}. aa is the largest odor of every other model read as well.

![Strength by odor at the megamat KC somata, odor by odor. Left: each odor's response rate as a multiple of the mean over the panel's odors, in the flies (mean and range of the four flies) and in the best family, as fitted and held at the flies' response rate (the mean view of the eight wirings); the odors are ordered by the flies' value. Right: aa along the four megamat stages, the flies (mean and range) and the family.](results/summary_stages/strength_by_odor_somata.png)

| validation2 | {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:view_error ~ .2f}} | {{E138/halves_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=fit;row=common:view_error ~ .2f}} / {{E138/halves_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=held;row=common:view_error ~ .2f}} | {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:first_odor}}, {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:second_odor}} | {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:first_share ~ .2f}} | {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:top2_share ~ .2f}} | {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:view_error_without_first ~ .2f}} |

**Megamat is concentrated through aa alone, and the held-out panels are not.** "Concentrated" was fixed beforehand: the two largest odors carry more than half of the squared error on both halves of the flies. On the APL16 panel and on validation2 the error is spread over four or more odors. The APL16 panel's largest odor is -aPine, whose input was recorded [at another concentration](#fn-concentrations) than the flies were given. The mixtures' shares follow the reading of the flies and are not read here.

**The errors are in which odors are strong, not in how widely the strengths spread.** On megamat the family spreads its response rates over the odors {{E138/entries_split.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} times as widely as the flies do, and its profile follows the flies' at a Pearson r of {{E138/entries_split.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:strength_rate_r ~ .2f}}; on validation2 the values are {{E138/entries_split.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} and {{E138/entries_split.csv:panel=validation2;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:strength_rate_r ~ .2f}}. A fit in the calyx that narrows or widens the spread does not reach an error of this kind.

aa along the megamat stages: its response rate as a multiple of the mean over the panel's odors, and its share of each stage's squared strength error.

| megamat stage | the flies' mean<br>(lowest to highest fly) | the best family,<br>as fitted | the best family,<br>held | aa's share of the stage's<br>strength error, as fitted |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PN dendrites | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_dendrite;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies ~ .2f}} ({{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_dendrite;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_min ~ .2f}} to {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_dendrite;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_max ~ .2f}}) | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_dendrite;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;stage=pn_dendrite;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_dendrite;row=common;odor=aa:view_share ~ .2f}} |
| PN boutons | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_bouton;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies ~ .2f}} ({{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_bouton;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_min ~ .2f}} to {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_bouton;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_max ~ .2f}}) | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_bouton;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;stage=pn_bouton;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=pn_bouton;row=common;odor=aa:view_share ~ .2f}} |
| KC claws | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_claw;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies ~ .2f}} ({{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_claw;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_min ~ .2f}} to {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_claw;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_max ~ .2f}}) | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_claw;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;stage=kc_claw;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_claw;row=common;odor=aa:view_share ~ .2f}} |
| KC somata | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_soma;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies ~ .2f}} ({{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_soma;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_min ~ .2f}} to {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_soma;row=common;odor=aa:rate_flies_max ~ .2f}}) | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_soma;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;stage=kc_soma;row=common;odor=aa:rate_model ~ .2f}} | {{E138/stages.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;stage=kc_soma;row=common;odor=aa:view_share ~ .2f}} |

**aa is lost between the claws and the somata.** In the flies aa rises from the dendrites to the somata. In the model it is above the flies up to the claws and falls below them at the somata. The error is made at the step from claws to somata, not upstream. What the flies and the model each do with aa at that step was read next ([below](#claw-soma-aa)).

**aa's input lies outside the dendrite recordings.** Its largest recorded inputs are DC4, DP1l and DL2v, which are fed by ORNs with ionotropic receptors. None is among the glomeruli of the PN-dendrite recordings, so the imposed dendrites left them as the model has them. DC4 has {{E138/classes_glomeruli.csv:glomerulus=DC4:hemibrain-first__n_boutons ~ .0f}} boutons in the hemibrain's calyx, so any term on it rests on very few units.

**One common cause was tested, and none was found.** Each odor's error, over the odors of megamat and of the APL16 panel, was regressed on the share of the odor's input that comes through a class of glomeruli. Three classes were fixed before any error was read. A class was to pass if its fit predicts validation2's errors at a correlation of {{const:experiments/E138_strength_by_odor_entries.py:REGRESSION_PASS_R}} or more, above {{const:experiments/E138_strength_by_odor_entries.py:REGRESSION_PASS_Q ~ .0%}} of {{const:experiments/E138_strength_by_odor_entries.py:N_RANDOM_CLASSES ~ ,d}} random classes of its size, on both halves of the flies.

The regression read on validation2's {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.ir.halves.fit.n_read}} odors. A cell gives the Pearson r between the fit's prediction and validation2's errors, on the first half of the flies / on the second. "Random classes" is the r that {{diff:lit:1 # all of the random classes, as a share -- const:experiments/E138_strength_by_odor_entries.py:REGRESSION_PASS_Q ~ .0%}} of the random classes exceed.

| class of glomeruli | glomeruli<br>in the class | r, the two halves<br>of the flies | random classes,<br>the two halves | passes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| fed by ORNs with ionotropic receptors | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:len(classes.ir.glomeruli)}} | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.ir.halves.fit.r ~ .2f}} / {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.ir.halves.held.r ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.ir.halves.fit.r_random_q95 ~ .2f}} / {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.ir.halves.held.r_random_q95 ~ .2f}} | no |
| absent from the PN-dendrite recordings | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:len(classes.not_dendrite28.glomeruli)}} | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.not_dendrite28.halves.fit.r ~ .2f}} / {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.not_dendrite28.halves.held.r ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.not_dendrite28.halves.fit.r_random_q95 ~ .2f}} / {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.not_dendrite28.halves.held.r_random_q95 ~ .2f}} | no |
| few in boutons or claws in the hemibrain | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:len(classes.few_hemibrain.glomeruli)}} | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.few_hemibrain.halves.fit.r ~ .2f}} / {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.few_hemibrain.halves.held.r ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.few_hemibrain.halves.fit.r_random_q95 ~ .2f}} / {{json:results/E138_strength_by_odor_entries/regression.json:classes.few_hemibrain.halves.held.r_random_q95 ~ .2f}} | no |

**No class passes on either half.** The nearest is the class absent from the dendrite recordings on the first half, and its fit turns over on the second. So megamat's concentration is aa's own, not a class's, and what a fit in the calyx could gain at the megamat somata by closing one odor's entry is the room stated above. The regression was read on validation2's odors, which costs that panel some of its standing as a held-out panel ([caveats](#caveats)).

**Limits.** There are four megamat soma flies, two to a half, and the halves agree through aa only: over the odors, the two halves' errors follow each other at a ρ of {{E138/halves_agreement.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_fitted;reading=e134;row=common:rate_resid__spearman ~ .2f}}. So "aa" is the finding, and no order of the other odors is.

### The best family fitted to all megamat stages at once {#stage-fit}

**The question was whether the family comes nearer the flies once the stages are in its loss,** and which of its structural choices the stages name. Until then the family's parameters had been set by the APL effect on the APL16 panel alone. In the best family fitted to all megamat stages at once, the loss of a fit is the megamat stage error on strength by odor.

**The design: {{json:results/E136_stage_population_fit/measure_strength/classes.json:len(classes) ~ w}} fits and a control, each read where it was not fitted.**

- **Fitted on one half, read on the other.** A fit is made on half of the family's eight wirings, against half of each stage's flies. It is read on the other wirings against the other flies, and the unrefitted family is read there too. Against half of the flies every model reads higher than against all of them, so the values of this section are compared with each other and not with the stage table.
- **Both response rates are held** at every step of a fit: the megamat flies' response rate with APL on, and each wiring's own response rate with APL off. A fit searches what the two rates leave free.
- **Each fit tests one structural choice.** The family refitted searches two scales of the calyx: that of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons and that of the inhibition among KCs. The others repeat that fit with one change each: the antennal lobe's normalization searched as well, APL's inhibition of the claws as a gain in place of a subtraction, a per-claw threshold searched as well, or the inhibition among KCs taken out.
- **The antennal lobe enters through two global terms.** The PN stages fitted to the recorded dendrites handed on no stage ([above](#antennal-lobe-dendrites)). So the fit with the antennal lobe's normalization searched has the additive term and the scale of the model's own normalization in its search, and no term per glomerulus.
- **The control fit** is the family refitted to flies whose odor labels are permuted. Its fall says what the freedom of a fit gains where there is nothing to learn.
- **A fit "holds"** if its megamat stage error falls by more than {{const:experiments/E136_stage_population_fit.py:IMPROVE_SE ~ .0f}} standard errors over the wirings and by more than the control fit's fall, and if nothing else gets worse by more than the unrefitted family's spread over its eight wirings. That covers every megamat stage, on strength by odor and over all its views, and the KC somata of the held-out panels of the test: validation2, the control mixtures as uploaded, and the APL16 panel with APL on and with APL off. The kiwi mixtures are read beside the test ([caveats](#caveats)).

Strength by odor at the megamat stages on wirings and flies that no fit saw, in one-fly units; lower is better. A value is the mean over the {{json:results/E136_stage_population_fit/measure_strength/classes.json:len(classes.A1.megamat.changes) ~ w}} held-out wirings, against the held-out half of each stage's flies. A change is from the unrefitted family there, and its standard error over the wirings is {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=se:value ~ .3f}}. The bar for a fit is a fall of {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}}, and the control fit falls by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A0_perm;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=control_fall:value ~ .3f}}.

| without the inhibition among KCs | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=kc_claw;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=stage_error_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} | {{json:results/E136_stage_population_fit/measure_strength/classes.json:classes.A4.cls}} |

**One fit holds as scored: the one with the antennal lobe's normalization searched.** Its megamat stage error is {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=stage_error_mean:value ~ .3f}} against the unrefitted family's {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=stage_error_mean:value ~ .3f}}, on wirings and flies that it did not see. The fall clears the bar and the control fit's fall, and the fit's highest wiring, at {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=stage_error_max:value ~ .3f}}, is below the family's lowest, at {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=stage_error_min:value ~ .3f}}. Against the family refitted it changes the megamat stage error by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_vs_A0:value ~ +.3f}}, and the error of the boutons, the claws and the somata alone by {{json:results/E136_stage_population_fit/measure_strength/classes.json:classes.A1.adds_downstream.change ~ +.3f}}.

**Every megamat stage comes nearer the flies, the stages before the calyx most.** The PN dendrites and the PN boutons fall furthest and the KC claws less, each by more than the family's spread at that stage. The KC somata change by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}, inside their spread. So what the fit gains before the calyx reaches the claws, and little of it reaches the somata. With aa's entry taken out, the somata's change is smaller still ([below](#claw-soma-aa)).

**With the flies resampled, the fall stands and the hold is a near-tie.** The fall of the megamat stage error is beyond its bar against both halves on every joint split of the flies, and the falls at the dendrites and the boutons are beyond theirs on nearly every draw. The claws' fall is beyond its bar on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals_changes.csv:table=E136_stage_population_fit;arm=A1;statistic=stage/kc_claw:share_improves ~ .2f}} of the draws, and the class "holds" is read on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E136_stage_population_fit;arm=A1:share_scored ~ .2f}} ([below](#fly-sampling)).

**Fitted again on the other wirings and against the other flies, the fall repeats and the hold does not.** The form of this fit was fitted in the {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/classes.json:len(experiment.cells_scored) ~ w}} other combinations of a half of the wirings with a half of each stage's flies. Where each is read, the megamat stage error changes by {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=X;stage=stage;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=read;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}, {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=F;stage=stage;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=read;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} and {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=W;stage=stage;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=read;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}: a fall beyond its bar and beyond the control fit's fall every time. None of them holds ([below](#stage-fit-again)). So "the fitted model", here and in the sections that follow, is one fit: its gain before the calyx has been found every time, and its hold once.

**The fitted model has not reached the flies.** Its megamat stage error is above what the fitted half of the flies predicts for the other half, {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=yardstick_half_to_half;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=stage_error:value ~ .3f}}, and far above the flies' floors of {{min:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=flies;statistic=floor:value ~ .2f}} to {{max:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=flies;statistic=floor:value ~ .2f}}.

**The fits that change only the calyx gain nothing that the control fit does not.** The family refitted, APL's inhibition of the claws as a gain and the per-claw threshold searched lower the megamat stage error by {{min:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm!=A1;arm!=A4;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=fall:value ~ .3f}} to {{max:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm!=A1;arm!=A4;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=fall:value ~ .3f}}, under the bar and under the control fit's fall. Each raises the somata's error over all three views by {{min:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm!=A1;arm!=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_all_views:value ~ +.3f}} to {{max:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm!=A1;arm!=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_all_views:value ~ +.3f}}, more than its spread of {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread_all_views:value ~ .3f}}, so none would pass with a larger fall.

**The megamat stages ask for no per-claw threshold.** The searched threshold ends at the bottom of its range. With the threshold taken out again the megamat stage error on the fitted wirings and flies rises by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A3;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=claw_thr_return_rise_stages:value ~ .3f}}, with no penalty, and that fit is then the family refitted at a second point.

**Without the inhibition among KCs the somata move away from the flies.** With both response rates held, the megamat somata's strength error rises by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}, and the somata of every held-out panel of the test rise by more than their spreads. So on the stage target too the family is nearer the flies with that inhibition than without it, although it has no named carrier in the calyx ([what carries over](#apl16-carryover)).

The searched factors of the fit with the antennal lobe's normalization searched, each as a multiple of the family's value, and what happens when one factor alone is returned to the family's value. The megamat stage error is read on the fitted wirings against the fitted half of the flies, where it is {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=classed_loss:value ~ .3f}} for the fit and {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=stage_error_S_fit:value ~ .3f}} for the unrefitted family. The penalty is what a fit's loss adds where a wiring's response rates cannot be held at their targets: one one-fly unit for every {{const:experiments/E136_stage_population_fit.py:HOLD_MISS_PER ~ .0%}} by which a rate misses its target. The loss of a fit is the stage error and the penalty together.

| | | when the factor alone is returned to the family's value {span=2} |
| searched factor | multiple of the family's value | rise of the megamat<br>stage error | penalty for response rates<br>that cannot be held |
|---|---|---|---|
| the additive term of the PN normalization | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhadd:value ~ .1f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhadd_return_rise_stages:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhadd_return_penalty:value ~ .3f}} |
| the scale of the PN normalization | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhsc:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhsc_return_rise_stages:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhsc_return_penalty:value ~ .3f}} |
| the scale of the inhibition among KCs | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=kckc:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=kckc_return_rise_stages:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=kckc_return_penalty:value ~ .3f}} |
| the scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=aplpn:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=aplpn_return_rise_stages:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=aplpn_return_penalty:value ~ .3f}} |

**The gain rests on one term of the antennal lobe's normalization.** In the model a PN's gain is a scale divided by the sum of an additive term and the summed input of all the glomeruli. The fit raises the additive term to many times the family's value, so a PN's gain depends much less on the other glomeruli: a weaker normalization. Returned alone to the family's value, that term gives back nearly all of the fit's gain: the stage error rises by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhadd_return_rise_stages:value ~ .3f}}, where the whole fit had gained {{diff:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=stage_error_S_fit:value -- E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=classed_loss:value ~ .3f}}.

**With that term returned, the response rates can no longer be held.** That is the penalty in the table, and it is not stage error. The scale of the normalization hardly moves the stage error, and its return costs the fit through the response rates alone. The two scales of the calyx each add a little to the stage error and cost no penalty.

The response-rate part of strength by odor at each megamat stage, split in two. "Spread" is the standard deviation of the model's response rates over the odors as a multiple of the flies', so 1 is the flies' spread. "Order" is the error that the spread does not explain, in one-fly units: it is zero when the model's profile over the odors is the flies' profile stretched about the panel's mean. Both are of the mean view of the held-out wirings, against the held-out half of the flies.

| | PN dendrites {span=2} | PN boutons {span=2} | KC claws {span=2} | KC somata {span=2} |
| model | spread | order | spread | order | spread | order | spread | order |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| the family, not refitted | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_claw;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_claw;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} |
| the antennal lobe's normalization searched | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_claw;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_claw;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=split_rate_order:value ~ .2f}} |

**The fit widens the spread of the odors' strengths and does not re-order them.** From the PN dendrites to the KC claws the fitted model's odors are spread more nearly as widely as the flies', though still less widely, and the order part hardly moves at any stage. At the somata neither part moves. That fits what the somata's error is made of: which odors are strong, not how widely the strengths spread ([above](#strength-entries)).

**This qualifies what the antennal lobe held to the recorded PN dendrites found.** There a PN stage nearer the flies' dendrites was a worse input to the unchanged calyx ([above](#antennal-lobe-dendrites)). Here the normalization changes for every glomerulus at once and two scales of the calyx are fitted with it, and the boutons and the claws do come nearer the flies. What carries into the calyx is the spread of the odors' strengths, which a global normalization sets, and not a pattern glomerulus by glomerulus.

**The recorded dendrites under the fitted model's calyx bear this out.** There the boutons get the flies' spread, keep the dendrites' order and move away from the flies ([below](#dendrite-bouton-step)).

**The fit's three changes were then read apart** ([below](#normalization-ladder)). The weaker normalization alone brings the dendrites, the boutons and the claws nearer the flies on megamat, and it moves the somata of validation2 and of the APL16 panel with APL on away from them. The two scales of the calyx are what keep those panels. The scale of the inhibition among KCs was then read along its dose and in other forms: only the hemibrain's matrix itself keeps the panels ([below](#kckc-rise)).

The KC somata of the held-out panels on strength by odor, in one-fly units; lower is better. A value is the mean over the held-out wirings, each wiring held at its response rates for that panel. Beside the test, the mixture panels are read by the position of the components in the session, each reading in the one-fly units of its own flies ([the held-out panels read like for like](#heldout-readings)).

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| the family, not refitted | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/cmix/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/off;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/kmix/on;condition=on;half=components_first_block;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/kmix/on;condition=on;half=components_second_block;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/cmix/on;condition=on;half=components_second_block;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} |
| its spread over the eight wirings | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/cmix/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/off;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/kmix/on;condition=on;half=components_first_block;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/kmix/on;condition=on;half=components_second_block;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/cmix/on;condition=on;half=components_second_block;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}} |
| the antennal lobe's normalization searched:<br>its change from the family | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/cmix/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/off;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/kmix/on;condition=on;half=components_first_block;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/kmix/on;condition=on;half=components_second_block;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/cmix/on;condition=on;half=components_second_block;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} |

**On the panels of the test the fitted model is neither better nor worse.** On validation2, on the control mixtures as uploaded and on the APL16 panel with APL on and with APL off, its somata change by less than the family's spread. Over all three views they change by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_all_views:value ~ +.3f}} on validation2 and by {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=mixtures/cmix/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_all_views:value ~ +.3f}} on the control mixtures. So the gain on megamat is not shown to carry to other odors, and it is not bought with them.

**That is as scored.** With the flies resampled the fitted model keeps these panels on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E136_stage_population_fit;arm=A1:share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws ([below](#fly-sampling)). In the other fits of the same form, made since, the APL16 panel's somata with APL on are worse than the family's spread every time ([below](#stage-fit-again)).

**Beside the test, one reading of the mixture flies puts it further from the flies.** Against the components' second block, the one after the mixtures, the fitted model is further from the flies than the family by more than the spread, on the kiwi mixtures and on the control mixtures. Against the kiwi components' first block it stays inside the spread. These readings gate nothing, and which of them reads a mixture session rightly is open ([caveats](#caveats)).

**Realism,** on the APL16 panel, which no fit saw, over all eight wirings. Both response rates are held on every wiring, so the bands on the response rate and on the ratio hold by construction.

- **One fit leaves a band.** With APL's inhibition of the claws as a gain, the response rate ρ with APL on falls to {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A2;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=rate_rho_on_min:value ~ .2f}} on the hemibrain's wiring, under the band's {{const:kcq/apl_effect.py:RATE_RHO_MIN ~ .2f}}. It is flagged, and it has no class in any case.
- **The fit with the antennal lobe's normalization searched stays inside all four bands on all eight wirings.** Its numbers of odors per KC are nearer the flies' than the family's. Its largest spike counts are higher: up to {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} with APL on and {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_off_max:value ~ .0f}} with APL off, against the family's {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} and {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_off_max:value ~ .0f}}.
- **Two of its parameters would be hard to defend.** The additive term of its PN normalization is {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=inhadd:value ~ .1f}} times the family's. The fly's antennal lobe is known to normalize a PN's response by the summed ORN input, so a normalization this weak would have to show in the recorded step from the ORNs to the PN dendrites. Read there, the flies' step shows no dependence on the summed input, and the recordings cannot tell the fitted model's stage from the family's ([below](#normalization-ladder)). Its scale of the inhibition among KCs, a term with no named carrier in the calyx, is {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=kckc:value ~ .2f}} times the family's.
- **Where APL acts is not fixed by the stages.** In the family, {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} of the drive that APL takes from the claws is taken at the PN boutons, where the connectome puts {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=connectome;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share:value ~ .3f}} of APL's calyx synapses. In the fits that keep the inhibition among KCs that share is {{min:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm!=family;arm!=A4;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} to {{max:E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm!=family;arm!=A4;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}}, and in the fit without it {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .3f}}. The family refitted makes that move and gains no more than the control fit, and the fit nearest the connectome's share is the one furthest from the flies.
- **Fitted again, the same form puts it elsewhere.** On other wirings and against other flies the form with the antennal lobe's normalization searched ends at {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=W;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}}, {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=X;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} and {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=F;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} of that drive taken at the PN boutons ([below](#stage-fit-again)).

The APL effect on the APL16 panel for every fit, a test on recordings that no fit of the stages saw: the mean over the eight wirings, read through the calibrated readout, each wiring scored by itself ([how the statistics are defined](#fn-apl-effect)). The family was fitted to these recordings, so its row is a fit. Lower is better for the loss and its parts.

**Every fit of the stages moves the APL effect away from the flies.** The pattern part rises in every fit, least in the three that change only the calyx and most without the inhibition among KCs. The fit with the antennal lobe's normalization searched is second to that one, and the furthest of the fits that keep the inhibition. Its rise is in the shape part, and its ρ on the per-pair APL effect is the lowest of any fit.

**The stage target and the APL effect pull apart at the antennal lobe.** The weaker normalization that brings the megamat stages nearer the flies takes the APL effect further from them, as a weaker normalization did in the earlier work ([above](#before-calyx)). Read apart, the weaker normalization alone raises the pattern part by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=apl_effect;half=T;statistic=price_value:value ~ +.4f}} on the held-out wirings, where the fitted model raises it by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=apl_effect;half=T;statistic=price_value:value ~ +.4f}}; the two calyx scales bring the rest ([below](#normalization-ladder)). Nothing here is fitted to the APL16 panel. Whether a model should be chosen with both in view is a decision for those who direct the project.

**As a whole, the megamat stage error orders the fits' evaluations alike against both halves of the flies.** Over the {{json:results/E136_stage_population_fit/measure_strength/classes.json:beside.consistency.0.n_evals ~ .0f}} saved evaluations of all the fits, its values against the fitted half follow those against the held-out half at a ρ of {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=all_arms;stage=stage_error;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_vs_held;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}}, above the {{json:results/E134_population_baseline/lock.json:rules.gate.B_reading.min_consistency}} asked of a measure. This is a description: the evaluations lie along the paths of a few searches and are not separate models. In the later fits of the form with the antennal lobe's normalization searched, the ρ over one fit's evaluations is {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=X;stage=fit;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=both;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}}, {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=F;stage=fit;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=both;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}} and {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=W;stage=fit;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=both;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}}, against {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=fit;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=both;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}} for the first ([below](#stage-fit-again)).

**The claws and the somata do not order models by themselves.** By stage the ρ is {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=all_arms;stage=pn_dendrite_error;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_vs_held;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}} at the dendrites, {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=all_arms;stage=pn_bouton_error;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_vs_held;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}} at the boutons, {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=all_arms;stage=kc_soma_error;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_vs_held;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}} at the somata and {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=all_arms;stage=kc_claw_error;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_vs_held;statistic=consistency_rho:value ~ .2f}} at the claws. A half holds two flies at each of the last two stages. Over {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:n_settings ~ .0f}} separate saved settings and every split of the flies the same holds ([below](#fly-sampling)).

**Limits.**

- **The control compares falls from different starts.** The control fit starts far from its permuted targets, and a fit with more room falls further. The fit with the antennal lobe's normalization searched clears it by a wide margin. The fits without a class also miss the bar, which does not depend on the control.
- **The antennal lobe was searched, not handed on.** The fitted PN stage is the model's own normalization with two global terms changed. It has no term per glomerulus and is not a stage fitted to the dendrites alone.
- **The flies are few after the dendrites.** A half is two flies at the claws and at the somata, and the one-fly units are those of all the stage's flies ([footnote](#fn-one-fly)).
- **Every fit here is one split's.** Each was made on one half of the wirings against one half of each stage's flies. The fit that holds as scored has since been made in the other combinations: its fall repeats in every one, and its hold in none ([below](#stage-fit-again)).
- **One search ran longer than planned.** The fit with the per-claw threshold searched was restarted and made more evaluations than its budget. It ended where the family refitted did.
- **Concentrations.** Every number of the APL16 panel involves paa, euc and -aPine ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)).

### The weaker antennal-lobe normalization without the calyx refit, along its strength {#normalization-ladder}

**The question was which of the fitted model's three changes carries its gain.** The one fit of the megamat stages that holds as scored joins three changes: a much larger additive term of the PN normalization, a smaller scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons and a larger scale of the inhibition among KCs ([above](#stage-fit)). The experiment is the weaker antennal-lobe normalization without the calyx refit, along its strength. It sets each change by itself, with nothing fitted and both response rates held, and reads every setting as the fit was read.

**The design.**

- **The normalization alone.** The family's own calyx is run at four values of the additive term: {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd100.rung ~ .0f}}, {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd314.rung ~ .0f}}, {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}} and {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd3141.rung ~ .0f}}. The larger the term, the weaker the normalization. The family's term is {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_family;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=pn_inhadd:value ~ .1f}} and the fitted model's {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_A1;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=pn_inhadd:value ~ .0f}}.
- **Each calyx scale alone.** The fit's scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons, {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=aplpn:value ~ .3f}} times the family's, is set alone at two of those terms. Its scale of the inhibition among KCs, {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=kckc:value ~ .2f}} times the family's, is set alone at one.
- **One refit, on a condition fixed beforehand.** If a setting at a term below the fitted model's improved at less than half of the fitted model's price, the family's two calyx scales were to be fitted again with the term fixed there. The term {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd314.rung ~ .0f}} met the condition.
- **The classes are the fit's** ([above](#stage-fit)), read on the held-out wirings against the held-out half of the flies, with the unrefitted family as the reference. A setting "improves" if its megamat stage error falls by more than {{const:experiments/E136_stage_population_fit.py:IMPROVE_SE ~ .0f}} standard errors over the wirings and no megamat stage gets worse by more than the family's spread. It "holds" if it improves and no held-out panel of the test gets worse by more than that spread, and it is "megamat only" if one does. It is "one stage at another's cost" if the megamat stage error falls and a megamat stage gets worse.
- **A price on the APL effect** is read beside the class. A setting has one if the pattern part of the APL-effect loss rises from the family's by more than {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:price_rule.threshold ~ .3f}}, against all the recordings or against either half of them.
- **A first step with no run** reads the recorded step from the ORNs to the PN dendrites, odor by odor, against each odor's summed ORN input.

The settings on wirings and flies that no fit saw, on strength by odor in one-fly units; lower is better. A value is the mean over the {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:len(classes.F_inhadd1000.megamat.changes) ~ w}} held-out wirings, megamat against the held-out half of the flies. A fall of the megamat stage error counts if it is more than {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}}, or {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd3141;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}} at the largest term. A held-out panel's cell is its KC somata's change from the unrefitted family, read against the family's spread in the second row. The rise of the pattern part is the largest of its readings against all the recordings and against each half of them.

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

**The outcome: the weaker normalization holds only together with both of the fit's calyx scales.** With the family's calyx no term holds. Neither calyx scale holds alone, and the refit at a smaller term does not. Every setting that improves has a price on the APL effect, so none serves the stages and the APL effect at once. That hold is the first fit's: fitted again on other wirings and against other flies, the form with both scales searched holds in none ([below](#stage-fit-again)).

**Alone, the weaker normalization gives most of the fitted model's gain on megamat, and all of it before the somata.** At {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}} the megamat stage error changes by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}, where the fitted model's changes by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}. The PN dendrites and the PN boutons change by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} and {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} and the KC claws by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=kc_claw;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}, each a fall beyond the family's spread at that stage. The KC somata change by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}. At the largest term the claws and the somata get worse by more than their spreads.

**It loses the held-out panels.** From {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd314.rung ~ .0f}} on, validation2's somata move away from the flies by more than the family's spread, and at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}} the APL16 panel's somata with APL on do as well. The fitted model, at nearly the same term, moves neither panel by more than its spread.

**Each calyx scale alone is megamat only as well, and the two do different things.** The bouton scale alone gives nearly all of the fitted model's fall on megamat at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}}, and it leaves validation2 and the APL16 panel with APL on worse, as the normalization alone does. The scale of the inhibition among KCs alone keeps validation2 and the control mixtures. With it only the APL16 panel's somata with APL on are worse, and by hardly more than their spread. With both scales no panel is worse.

**So the fitted model holds because its calyx scales undo, on the held-out panels, what its normalization does to them.** The scale that keeps validation2 is that of the inhibition among KCs, a term with no named carrier in the calyx ([what carries over](#apl16-carryover)). Its dose and its form were read next ([below](#kckc-rise)).

**Two of these classes are near-ties when the flies are resampled.** The scale of the inhibition among KCs alone is megamat only on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E139_normalization_ladder;arm=K_inhadd1000:share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws, and the fitted model holds on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E139_normalization_ladder;arm=A1:share_scored ~ .2f}}. So the difference between that scale alone and both scales is not settled by these flies. The bouton scale alone stays megamat only on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E139_normalization_ladder;arm=B_inhadd1000:share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws, and the normalization alone at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd314.rung ~ .0f}} on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E139_normalization_ladder;arm=F_inhadd314:share_scored ~ .2f}} ([below](#fly-sampling)).

**The megamat stages ask for that larger scale only at the fitted model's term.** With the additive term fixed at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd314.rung ~ .0f}}, the refit fixes the bouton scale, at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/fit_A0_inhadd314.json:factors.aplpn ~ .3f}} times the family's, and returns the scale of the inhibition among KCs to the family's value. It then reads as the bouton scale alone does at that term: megamat only, and about a standard error below the normalization alone on megamat. So nothing read at a term below the fitted model's holds. At terms as large as the fitted model's or larger, every later fit of the full form asks for the larger scale again ([below](#stage-fit-again)).

**At the somata the gain on the other odors is lost on aa.** With the family's calyx at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}}, the somata's error without aa's entry falls from the family's {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} to {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}}, further than the fitted model's {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}}. aa's share of the somata's squared error rises from {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=aa_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} to {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=aa_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} meanwhile. The fit's calyx scales give up most of that gain and keep aa's share at the family's. A single odor's share is a lead, and what lifts aa at the flies' somata is still open ([below](#claw-soma-aa)).

The APL effect on the APL16 panel for the same settings, a test on recordings that no fit of the stages saw: the mean over the eight wirings, read through the calibrated readout, each wiring scored by itself ([how the statistics are defined](#fn-apl-effect)). The family was fitted to these recordings, so its row is a fit. Lower is better for the pattern part and its parts.

**Every setting that improves has a price, and the two kinds of change pay it differently.** With the normalization alone the pattern part rises as the model's effect spreads more widely over the pairs, and the two correlations fall little. Either calyx scale adds to the rise with hardly a wider spread: the correlations fall, furthest in the fitted model. At {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}} the normalization alone raises the pattern part by {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=apl_effect;half=T;statistic=price_value:value ~ +.4f}} of the fitted model's {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=apl_effect;half=T;statistic=price_value:value ~ +.4f}} (the first table). These rows describe a held-out test, and no setting is chosen by them.

**The flies' own step from the ORNs to the PN dendrites does not show the normalization's signature.** A normalization by the summed input turns down most the odors with the most summed ORN input. So each odor's residual off a proportional step from the ORNs to the dendrites was set against its summed input, over the panel's odors. In the flies the two are unrelated, at a ρ of {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=flies;stage=orn_to_pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=signature_rho_with_total:value ~ +.2f}} (90 % interval {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=flies;stage=orn_to_pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=signature_rho_lo:value ~ +.2f}} to {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=flies;stage=orn_to_pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=signature_rho_hi:value ~ +.2f}}). In the model's PN stage they follow each other at {{min:E139/writeup_values.csv:arm!=flies;stage=orn_to_pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=signature_rho_with_total:value ~ +.2f}} to {{max:E139/writeup_values.csv:arm!=flies;stage=orn_to_pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=signature_rho_with_total:value ~ +.2f}}, at every value of the additive term.

**A larger additive term shrinks the model's residuals and does not re-order them.** The spread of the odors' totals at the model's dendrites ([above](#before-calyx)) rises from the family's {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_family;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=totals_cv:value ~ .3f}} to {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_inhadd1000;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=totals_cv:value ~ .3f}} at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}}, against the flies' {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=flies;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=totals_cv:value ~ .3f}}. The family's and the fitted model's steps cannot be told apart through the flies' noise, so the recordings neither name the fitted term nor rule it out. What they do say is that the flies' step, over these odors, does not depend on the summed input as a global normalization makes it.

**Beside the classes, two readings of the earlier work were repeated at the fitted model's term** (the antennal lobe's normalization at the strength the flies' APL asks for). The model's APL then falls with concentration inside the flies' interval under one of that work's two readings. With APL on the model's KCs respond less at a low concentration, further below the flies'. Both rest on estimates of the input at low concentrations, the first on paa's, and they gate nothing.

**Realism.** No setting leaves a realism band on the APL16 panel on any wiring, and both response rates are held in every run.

- **The bouton scale moves APL's inhibition toward the connectome.** In the family {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} of the drive that APL takes from the claws is taken at the PN boutons, and with the normalization alone at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}} it is {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}}. With the fit's bouton scale alone it is {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} there, against the connectome's {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=connectome;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share:value ~ .3f}} of APL's calyx synapses.
- **Two parameters stay hard to defend.** In the model a PN's gain is a scale divided by the sum of the additive term and the summed input. At {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}} the term is {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_inhadd1000;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=inhadd_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} of that sum on the megamat input, against {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_family;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=inhadd_share_mean:value ~ .3f}} at the family's term. The other is the scale of the inhibition among KCs at {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=kckc:value ~ .2f}} times the family's, and it is the one that the held-out panels need.
- **The largest spike counts grow.** With APL on a KC's largest count reaches {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=F_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} with the normalization alone at {{json:results/E139_normalization_ladder/classes.json:classes.F_inhadd1000.rung ~ .0f}} and {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=K_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} with the larger scale of the inhibition among KCs, against the family's {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}}.
- **The cost of the gains is not in the bands.** It is the price on the APL effect, which every setting that improves pays.

**Limits.**

- **The fixed settings fit nothing, so they have no control fit.** Their classes rest on the bar, the family's spreads and the guard on the other views. The refit was run without a control fit as well, and it is read only where it was not fitted.
- **The APL16 values of the family's calyx along the term are saved runs** of the antennal lobe's normalization at the strength the flies' APL asks for. There the breadth that weights APL was computed again at each term, where the other settings keep the breadth of the fit.
- **The flies are few after the dendrites,** as for the fit ([above](#stage-fit)).
- **Validation2 is held out here.** Nothing in these settings was chosen from the regression that was read on its odors ([caveats](#caveats)).
- **Concentrations.** Every number of the APL16 panel involves paa, euc and -aPine ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)).

### The rise of the inhibition among KCs beside the weaker antennal-lobe normalization, along its dose and in other forms {#kckc-rise}

**The question was what the fitted model's hold on the held-out panels rests on.** The fitted model keeps those panels only with its inhibition among KCs at {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=factor;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=fit_S;statistic=kckc:value ~ .2f}} times the family's scale, and that inhibition has no named carrier in the calyx ([above](#normalization-ladder)). The experiment is the rise of the inhibition among KCs beside the weaker antennal-lobe normalization, along its dose and in other forms. It asks how large that rise has to be, whether it has to be there with APL off, and whether another form of inhibition can take its place.

**The design.**

- **One base point, and nothing fitted.** Every setting starts from the additive term {{json:results/E141_kckc_rise_forms/classes.json:base_point.rung ~ .0f}} with the fit's scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons, which by itself is megamat only ([above](#normalization-ladder)). Each adds its own form of the inhibition among KCs there. Both response rates are held, and every setting is read as the fit was read. The fitted model stands beside, at its own term of {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_A1;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=pn_inhadd:value ~ .0f}}.
- **The dose.** The family's inhibition among KCs is a matrix of weights between pairs of KCs, the hemibrain's synapse counts between them, times one scale. That matrix is set at {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .1f}} and at {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .2f}} times the family's scale, beside the family's scale and the fitted model's {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .2f}}.
- **The rise with APL on only.** The matrix is at the fitted model's scale with APL on and at the family's scale with APL off. This asks whether the rise has to be there with APL off.
- **A gain at the claws in its place.** APL's inhibition of the claws is a gain in place of a subtraction, and the matrix stays at the family's scale. This asks whether the rise stands for the form of APL's inhibition.
- **Three forms that keep the summed weight and not the pairs.** The first is one pooled neuron, through which every KC inhibits every KC alike, and the second is one such neuron for each KC type. The third is the matrix shuffled within pairs of KC types: each pair of types keeps its weights, on other pairs of KCs of those types. Each has the summed weight of the matrix at the fitted model's scale.
- **The classes are those of the weaker normalization's settings** ([above](#normalization-ladder)), read on the held-out wirings against the held-out half of the flies, with the unrefitted family as the reference. A setting is "strength at another view's cost" if its megamat stage error falls and a megamat stage's error over all its views gets worse by more than the family's spread. It has no class if a wiring leaves a realism band, or if its response rates cannot be held.
- **Two comparisons with the fitted model were fixed beforehand.** A dose "does more" than the fitted model if it holds and its megamat stage error is lower by more than the bar, or a held-out panel is better by more than its spread with none worse. A setting has "a lower price" if its pattern part of the APL-effect loss is below the fitted model's by more than {{json:results/E141_kckc_rise_forms/classes.json:price_rule.lower_than_a1 ~ .3f}}, against all the recordings and against each half of them.

The settings on wirings and flies that no fit saw, on strength by odor in one-fly units; lower is better. A value is the mean over the {{json:results/E141_kckc_rise_forms/classes.json:len(classes.N_inhadd1000.megamat.changes) ~ w}} held-out wirings, megamat against the held-out half of the flies. A fall of the megamat stage error counts if it is more than {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}}, or {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=S_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}} for the shuffled matrix.

A held-out panel's cell is its KC somata's change from the unrefitted family, read against the family's spread in the second row. The rise of the pattern part is from the family's, on the held-out wirings against all the recordings. The section above gives the largest of three readings, so the fitted model's value differs slightly there.
