**The outcome: only the hemibrain's matrix itself keeps the held-out panels, and no dose does more than the fitted model's.** From {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .1f}} to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .2f}} times the family's scale no held-out panel is worse than its spread. That is as scored: with the flies resampled each of these holds is a near-tie (the limits of this section). No other form stands in for the matrix: each loses a held-out panel, another view or its response rates. So the fitted model rests on the hemibrain's counts of synapses between KCs, read as an inhibition and scaled up.

**Megamat does not choose a dose.** The megamat stage error is the same along the whole ladder, to a fraction of the bar: its fall is the normalization's and the bouton scale's ([above](#normalization-ladder)). Against the fitted model the two new doses change it by {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_from_a1:value ~ +.3f}} and {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_from_a1:value ~ +.3f}}, with a standard error of {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=se:value ~ .3f}}. No held-out panel is better than the fitted model's by more than its spread. The fits made again say the same: the fitted scale ends at {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=F;stage=fit;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=b;statistic=best_kckc:value ~ .2f}}, at {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=W;stage=fit;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=a;statistic=best_kckc:value ~ .2f}} and at the top of the search's range, {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:fit.bounds.kckc.1 ~ .1f}}, a rise every time and at no one dose ([below](#stage-fit-again)).

**The held-out panels set a lower end, and it is a near-tie.** At the family's scale validation2's somata and the APL16 panel's somata with APL on are worse than their spreads. At {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .1f}} times that scale both are inside, the APL16 panel's by {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=margin_to_spread:value ~ .5f}}. The lower end thus lies between the two doses as scored. With the flies resampled the hold at {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .1f}} is read on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms;arm=D1.8_inhadd1000:share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws, the APL16 panel's somata with APL on being worse than their spread on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals_changes.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms;arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;statistic=panel/apl16/apl16/on:share_worse ~ .2f}}. So these flies do not place the lower end ([below](#fly-sampling)).

**Above the lower end, two held-out readings move apart.** Validation2's somata come nearer the flies along the whole ladder, and the APL16 panel's somata with APL on level off from the fitted model's scale. The APL effect moves away from the flies meanwhile (the second table). So a dose inside the range is a choice between two held-out readings, and that is a decision for those who direct the project.

**What the rise changes at the somata is how widely the odors' response rates spread, not which odors are strong.** The weaker normalization widens that spread on every panel, and the rise narrows it again. On validation2 the model's spread is {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2;condition=on;half=all;statistic=sd_ratio_mean_T:value ~ .2f}} times the flies' in the unrefitted family, {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2;condition=on;half=all;statistic=sd_ratio_mean_T:value ~ .2f}} at the base point with the family's scale and {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2;condition=on;half=all;statistic=sd_ratio_mean_T:value ~ .2f}} in the fitted model: too wide throughout, so the rise helps. On the APL16 panel with APL on the three values are {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=sd_ratio_mean_T:value ~ .2f}}, {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=sd_ratio_mean_T:value ~ .2f}} and {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=sd_ratio_mean_T:value ~ .2f}}: the model is already narrower than the flies there, and the rise takes it further.

**The rise has to be there with APL off as well.** With the rise with APL on only, the APL16 panel's somata with APL on are worse than their spread. The reason lies in how the response rates are held: the rate with APL off sets the KCs' threshold. A rise in both conditions lets that threshold fall, while a rise with APL on only leaves it high, and APL is then held weaker. So the rise works in part through the lower threshold that it allows.

**A gain at the claws is not the rise.** It gives the lowest megamat stage error of the settings read, within a standard error of the fitted model's. But the megamat somata's error over all three views rises by {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=G_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_all_views:value ~ +.3f}} against a spread of {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread_all_views:value ~ .3f}}, and validation2's somata are worse than their spread. Its class is strength at another view's cost.

**A pooled inhibition cannot hold the response rates.** With one neuron for all KCs or one for each KC type, at the matrix's summed weight, the response rate with APL off stays far below its target at the lowest threshold that the search allows. Both forms were stopped after their first reads and have no class. In the earlier work, too, no pooled neuron kept what the matrix gives the family on the APL effect.

**The shuffled matrix shows that the matrix's own structure matters, and not which part of it.** It keeps the weights of each pair of KC types and moves them onto other pairs of KCs of those types. That changes more than which KCs inhibit which. By a later reading of the matrices, the shuffle also makes the summed inhibition that each KC receives more even across the KCs, and removes nearly all of the matrix's reciprocity (entries whose reverse entry is also there) and of its preference for pairs of KCs that share an input glomerulus ([below](#male-calyces)).

**The shuffled matrix loses the APL16 panel with APL on and keeps validation2.** It fails at the APL16 panel's somata with APL on, and it leaves the band on response rate ρ on one wiring, so it has no class. On validation2 its somata change by {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=S_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=validation2/validation2/on;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_from_a1:value ~ +.3f}} from the fitted model's, a gain, and it alone has a lower price than the fitted model. So the matrix's own structure matters, at least on the APL16 panel. Which part of it carries the hold is not separated: the pairs themselves, the unevenness of the KCs' summed input, the reciprocity or the preference for shared input.

The APL effect on the APL16 panel for the same settings, a test on recordings that no fit of the stages saw: the mean over the eight wirings, read through the calibrated readout, each wiring scored by itself ([how the statistics are defined](#fn-apl-effect)). The family was fitted to these recordings, so its row is a fit. Lower is better for the pattern part and its parts.

**Every setting pays a price on the APL effect, and along the dose it grows.** The pattern part rises with the scale of the matrix, in its shape part. The model's spread of the effect over the pairs hardly changes along the dose, and both correlations fall, furthest at the largest dose. The mean APL effect falls along the dose as well, to below the flies'. These rows describe a held-out test, and no setting is chosen by them.

**At the megamat somata the rise gives back the gain on the other odors.** With aa's entry the somata's error hardly changes along the dose. Without it the error rises from {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} at the family's scale to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} at the largest dose, where the unrefitted family has {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}}. aa's share of the somata's squared error falls from {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=aa_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=aa_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} meanwhile. A single odor's share is a lead ([below](#claw-soma-aa)).

**Beside the test, no dose mends the mixture readings.** Read as uploaded, the kiwi mixtures' somata are worse than their spread at the two lowest doses and inside it from the fitted model's scale. Against the components' second block every setting is further from the flies than the family by more than the spread, on the kiwi mixtures and on the control mixtures. These readings gate nothing ([caveats](#caveats)).

**Beside the classes, the two readings of the earlier work were repeated at both new doses** (the antennal lobe's normalization at the strength the flies' APL asks for). At every dose the model's APL falls with concentration inside the flies' interval under one of that work's two readings, as it does in the fitted model: that is the normalization's doing.

**At a low concentration the model's KCs respond more as the rise grows, and still too little.** With APL on their response rate is {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=extended;condition=on;half=all;statistic=extended_low_on:value ~ .3f}} at the family's scale and {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=extended;condition=on;half=all;statistic=extended_low_on:value ~ .3f}} at the largest dose, below the unrefitted family's {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=extended;condition=on;half=all;statistic=extended_low_on:value ~ .3f}} and the flies' {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=extended;condition=on;half=all;statistic=extended_low_flies_on:value ~ .3f}}. Both readings rest on estimates of the input at low concentrations, and they gate nothing.

**Realism.** On the APL16 panel no setting that holds leaves a realism band on any wiring, and both response rates are held in every run.

- **The shuffled matrix leaves a band.** Its response rate ρ with APL on is {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=S_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=rate_rho_on_min:value ~ .2f}} on one wiring, under the band's {{const:kcq/apl_effect.py:RATE_RHO_MIN ~ .2f}}. It is flagged.
- **The largest dose would be hard to defend.** At {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .2f}} times the family's, the scale of the inhibition among KCs is {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=wKCKC_scale_min:value ~ .0f}} to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=wKCKC_scale_max:value ~ .0f}} over the wirings, against the family's {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=wKCKC_scale_min:value ~ .0f}} to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=wKCKC_scale_max:value ~ .0f}} and the fitted model's {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=wKCKC_scale_min:value ~ .0f}} to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=on;half=all;statistic=wKCKC_scale_max:value ~ .0f}}, for a term with no named carrier. The least dose that holds is {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .1f}} times the family's.
- **The KCs' threshold falls and their largest spike counts rise with the dose.** On megamat the threshold is {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=megamat_reads;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=fixed_thr_min:value ~ .0f}} to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=megamat_reads;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=fixed_thr_max:value ~ .0f}} over the wirings at the family's scale and {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=megamat_reads;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=fixed_thr_min:value ~ .0f}} to {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=megamat_reads;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=all;statistic=fixed_thr_max:value ~ .0f}} at the largest dose. With APL on, a KC's largest count on the APL16 panel reaches {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=B_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} at the family's scale and {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} at the largest dose, against the unrefitted family's {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16;condition=realism;half=all;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}}.
- **The gain at the claws is nearest the connectome on where APL acts.** With it {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=G_inhadd1000;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} of the drive that APL takes from the claws is taken at the PN boutons. With the matrix, in any form read here, that share is {{min:E141/writeup_values.csv:arm!=family;arm!=G_inhadd1000;arm!=K_inhadd1000;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} to {{max:E141/writeup_values.csv:arm!=family;arm!=G_inhadd1000;arm!=K_inhadd1000;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}}, and the connectome puts {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=connectome;stage=calyx;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=bouton_share:value ~ .3f}} of APL's calyx synapses there.
- **The cost of the rise is not in the bands.** It is the price on the APL effect, which grows with the dose.

**Limits.**

- **The doses' classes are as scored, and each hold is a near-tie when the flies are resampled.** With each stage's and each panel's flies drawn anew, the hold at {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .1f}} times the family's scale is read on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms;arm=D1.8_inhadd1000:share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws, the hold at {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D5.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .2f}} times on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms;arm=D5.8_inhadd1000:share_scored ~ .2f}} and the fitted model's on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms;arm=A1:share_scored ~ .2f}}. On the other draws a held-out panel or a megamat stage is worse than its spread. So the range of doses has no settled edge at either end: the plateau between the two doses is the reading, and neither end is a threshold ([below](#fly-sampling)).
- **Two verdicts do not turn on the flies.** That no dose does more than the fitted model's is read on {{min:E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms:does_more_than_a1_share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws or more, and that no other form holds in place of the matrix on {{min:E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms:stands_in_share_scored ~ .2f}} or more.
- **The reading of the rise with APL on only rests on holding both response rates.** Under a rule that held the rate with APL on alone, it could read otherwise.
- **The pooled forms were read at one summed weight,** that of the matrix at the fitted model's scale. A lower summed weight was not tried.
- **The doses stand at the base point's term and the fitted model at its own,** {{json:results/E141_kckc_rise_forms/classes.json:base_point.rung ~ .0f}} and {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_A1;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=pn_inhadd:value ~ .0f}}. That difference was not read by itself.
- **The same held-out panels judged {{json:results/E141_kckc_rise_forms/classes.json:arms_read ~ w}} settings here,** and validation2 has served once as a test set ([caveats](#caveats)). Nothing here was chosen from that regression, and no dose or form becomes a base from its class alone.
- **One mapped brain here.** The matrix and all eight wirings are the hemibrain's. The same settings have since been read on the male CNS's two calyces, each with its own matrix. Megamat falls there as well, the fitted model's factor holds on neither calyx, and one dose holds on one of them by a near-tie ([below](#male-calyces)).
- **The flies are few after the dendrites,** as for the fit ([above](#stage-fit)).
- **Concentrations.** Every number of the APL16 panel involves paa, euc and -aPine ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)).

### aa at the step from the claws to the somata {#claw-soma-aa}

**The question was what happens to aa between the claws and the somata.** aa carries most of the family's strength error at the megamat somata, and the model loses it at that one step ([above](#strength-entries)). The experiment is aa at the step from the claws to the somata. It ran no model. It read aa in the recordings of every megamat stage, in the saved megamat runs of the family and of its variants, and in the measurements of aa's input.

**Every reading and every class was fixed before any statistic of aa was read.** The classes are in the table below. The first one decided whether new runs were made: aa's input taken from each ORN fly's own recording, with nothing searched.

**The outcome: by the classes, the claws do not account for the loss and the step itself differs, and the reading of the somata has a share.** Both verdicts are close calls. The first class misses by one claw recording, so the runs were not made. The last class holds under one reading of the somata and under no other.

The classes as they were fixed beforehand, and what was read. "The lowest third" and "the highest third" are of an odor's rank among the panel's {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=kc_soma:n_odors ~ .0f}} odors.

| class | what it asks | what was read | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|

aa at the megamat claws and somata. A response rate is a multiple of the mean over the panel's odors, and a rank, in parentheses, is among those odors, 1 the lowest. "Concentration" is the share of the responders' summed response that the strongest tenth of them carry: it is high where few units carry most of the response. "Odors per responder" is the mean number of the panel's odors that a KC responding to aa responds to. A model's value is the mean over its wirings with APL on, read as the flies are, and its ranks are among its own odors.

**At the claws the flies and the model are opposite on concentration, and only partly on response rate.** In every claw recording aa is among the odors whose response is most concentrated in few regions. In the model it is among the least concentrated, on every wiring. The model's claws also give aa one of the highest response rates of the panel. The flies' claws give it a low one in {{json:results/E140_aa_claw_soma_step/classes.json:i.flies_count ~ w}} of the {{json:results/E140_aa_claw_soma_step/classes.json:i.flies_n ~ w}} recordings and a middling one in the others, where the class asked for {{json:results/E140_aa_claw_soma_step/lock.json:classes.i.flies_min ~ w}}.

![Each odor's response rate at the KC claws against the concentration of its responders. Left: the flies' claw recordings, one dot per odor and recording. Right: the best family, held at the megamat flies' response rate, one dot per odor and wiring. aa is in orange.](results/E140_aa_claw_soma_step/fig_claws_rate_concentration.png)

**From the claws to the somata the two sides move aa in opposite directions.** In the flies' means no odor rises more at that step, and the fly recorded at both sites on the same trials shows the rise as well. In the family aa falls from near the top of the panel to near the bottom.

**No saved run lifts aa at the somata.** The fits of the megamat stages that keep the inhibition among KCs read {{min:E140_aa_claw_soma_step/models_entries.csv:condition=on;stage=kc_soma;reading=curve_noise;wiring=common;odor=aa;group!=family_held;group!=family_fitted;group!=A4;group!=e135_imposed:rate ~ .2f}} to {{max:E140_aa_claw_soma_step/models_entries.csv:condition=on;stage=kc_soma;reading=curve_noise;wiring=common;odor=aa;group!=family_held;group!=family_fitted;group!=A4;group!=e135_imposed:rate ~ .2f}} of the panel's mean, and the family with APL off reads {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/models_entries.csv:group=family_held;condition=off;stage=kc_soma;reading=curve_noise;wiring=common;odor=aa:rate ~ .2f}}. All are under the lowest soma fly's {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_entries.csv:stage=kc_soma;cohort=wild_type;recording=mean;odor=aa:rate_min ~ .2f}}. Without the inhibition among KCs, and with the recorded dendrites imposed, aa is lower still (the table).

**The model's somata put aa where its summed input puts it, and the flies' somata lift it far above.** An odor's summed input is its ORN input to the model, summed over the glomeruli. Over the panel's other odors the somata's response rates follow it about as closely in the flies as in the model.

What the somata's response rates follow over the panel's odors. A line is fitted over the odors other than aa. "Predicted" is the line's value for aa, and the residual is aa's value minus it, with the same in standard deviations of the other odors' residuals in parentheses. The last column is aa's place among the panel's odors by the size of its residual when each odor in turn is left out of the fit, 1 the largest.

![The KC somata's response rate by odor, as a multiple of the panel's mean. Left: the best family, held at the megamat flies' response rate, against each odor's summed input. Right: the flies, against the response rate at their own claws. The line is fitted over the odors other than aa, and aa is in orange.](results/E140_aa_claw_soma_step/fig_soma_follows.png)

**aa is the one odor that the flies lift above its input.** Its residual in the flies is the largest of the panel, against the summed input and against the flies' own boutons. The odors with less summed input than aa lie near the line on both sides. In the model aa lies on the line as they do, and the model's somata hardly follow its own claws.

**The two sides recruit different KCs for aa.** The flies' aa responders are among the most selective of any odor's, and the family's are in the middle of its panel (the table above). The family's aa responders draw {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/models_anatomy.csv:group=family_held;condition=on;wiring=mean;odor=aa:ir_claw_share_responders ~ .2f}} of their claws from the glomeruli fed by ORNs with ionotropic receptors, against {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/models_anatomy.csv:group=family_held;condition=on;wiring=mean;odor=aa:ir_claw_share_all ~ .2f}} for all its KCs. That is the largest such share of any odor. With the recorded dendrites imposed the model's aa responders are selective by rank, and aa's response rate at the somata does not rise.

**The reading's share is narrow.** At the criterion of the APL16 panel the megamat soma flies respond at {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_soma_criteria.csv:cohort=megamat, Remy's four flies:rate_mean_at_apl16_criterion ~ .3f}} in place of {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_soma_criteria.csv:cohort=megamat, Remy's four flies:rate_mean ~ .4f}}, and aa's response rate falls to {{json:results/E140_aa_claw_soma_step/classes.json:iv.readings.criterion_apl16.rate ~ .2f}} of the panel's mean, out of the highest third. Under every other reading that counts it stays there: the reading fixed for the stage, each of the three presentations alone, each half of the response window, and the responder calls of the flies' experimenter. aa's mean response, which needs no criterion, keeps rank {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_soma_readings.csv:reading=criterion_apl16;recording=mean;odor=aa:mean_rank ~ g}}. So aa's strength at the somata holds under every reading at the flies' response rate.

**aa's input is a mean of unlike flies.** The model's input for aa is close to the mean of the {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_entries.csv:stage=orn;cohort=wild_type;recording=mean;odor=aa:n_recordings ~ w}} ORN flies that were given aa: the two follow each other at a Pearson r of {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/input_summary.csv:source=orn_mean;on=model36:pearson_with_ref ~ .2f}} over the glomeruli. The single flies' summed inputs run from {{min:E140_aa_claw_soma_step/input_summary.csv:on=model36;source!=orn_mean:sum ~ .0f}} to {{max:E140_aa_claw_soma_step/input_summary.csv:on=model36;source!=orn_mean:sum ~ .0f}} Hz, against the model's {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/input_summary.csv:source=model_input;n_glomeruli=36:sum ~ .0f}} Hz, and each is spread over fewer glomeruli than the model's input. As an effective number of glomeruli, the number that would carry the input if they shared it evenly, the flies read {{min:E140_aa_claw_soma_step/input_summary.csv:on=model36;source!=orn_mean:breadth ~ .1f}} to {{max:E140_aa_claw_soma_step/input_summary.csv:on=model36;source!=orn_mean:breadth ~ .1f}} and the model's input {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/input_summary.csv:source=model_input;n_glomeruli=36:breadth ~ .1f}}. Whether one fly's own aa would lift the model's somata is what the runs that were not made would have read.

**What it leaves.** The claws do not settle it: the flies' claws carry aa in few strong regions, and yet they are not short of responding regions in every recording. The step is clearer. The flies' somata give aa more than its input, its boutons or its claws predict, through KCs that respond to few odors. The model's somata give aa what its summed input predicts, through broadly tuned KCs with many claws on the glomeruli fed by ORNs with ionotropic receptors. These are descriptions of one odor, so they are leads for a proposal and not results.

**The megamat somata's strength error is read with and without aa's entry.** "Without aa's entry" takes aa's term out of the squared error and leaves the other odors' entries as they are ([above](#strength-entries)). For the family held, against all the flies, the error is {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:view_error ~ .2f}} with aa's entry and {{E138/entries_summary.csv:panel=megamat;condition=on;group=family_held;reading=e134;flies=all;row=common:view_error_without_first ~ .2f}} without it. The fits of the megamat stages were read the same way ([above](#stage-fit)).

The megamat KC somata's strength error with and without aa's entry, on wirings and flies that no fit saw, in one-fly units; lower is better. A value is the mean over the held-out wirings against the held-out half of the flies, and a change is from the unrefitted family there. The family's spread over its eight wirings at the somata is {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread:value ~ .3f}}.

| model | with aa's entry | without aa's entry | change from the family,<br>with aa's entry | change from the family,<br>without aa's entry |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| the family, not refitted | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_with_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | — | — |
| the family refitted | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A0;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_with_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A0;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A0;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_with_aa:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A0;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_without_aa:value ~ +.3f}} |
| the antennal lobe's normalization searched | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_with_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_with_aa:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_without_aa:value ~ +.3f}} |
| APL's inhibition of the claws as a gain | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A2;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_with_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A2;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A2;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_with_aa:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A2;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_without_aa:value ~ +.3f}} |
| the per-claw threshold searched | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A3;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_with_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A3;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A3;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_with_aa:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A3;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_without_aa:value ~ +.3f}} |
| without the inhibition among KCs | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_with_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_without_aa_mean:value ~ .3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_with_aa:value ~ +.3f}} | {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A4;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=change_without_aa:value ~ +.3f}} |

**What the fits of the calyx gain at the somata is aa's entry.** With aa's entry taken out, the family refitted, APL's inhibition of the claws as a gain and the per-claw threshold searched leave the somata further from the flies than the unrefitted family. The fit with the antennal lobe's normalization searched keeps a smaller fall. All of these changes are inside the family's spread at the somata. Without the inhibition among KCs most of the rise is in aa's entry, and what is left is still more than the spread.

**Realism.** No model was run or changed. In their saved runs the family and the fitted model are inside the four realism bands on the APL16 panel on every wiring, and what would be hard to defend in each is as stated [above](#best-models-table). Every number of the APL16 panel involves paa, euc and -aPine ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)). One caution is aa's own: its largest recorded inputs reach the calyx through very few boutons ([above](#strength-entries)), so a term for that input would rest on few units.

**Limits.**

- **One odor.** aa was read because its share of the error holds on both halves of the flies. What was read about it describes one odor.
- **Few flies.** There are {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_entries.csv:stage=kc_soma;cohort=wild_type;recording=mean;odor=aa:n_recordings ~ w}} soma flies, and aa's response rate runs from {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_entries.csv:stage=kc_soma;cohort=wild_type;recording=mean;odor=aa:rate_min ~ .2f}} to {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_entries.csv:stage=kc_soma;cohort=wild_type;recording=mean;odor=aa:rate_max ~ .2f}} of the panel's mean over them. With each fly at its own criterion the mean is {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_soma_readings.csv:reading=per_fly_level;recording=mean;odor=aa:rate ~ .2f}}, at rank {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_soma_readings.csv:reading=per_fly_level;recording=mean;odor=aa:rate_rank ~ g}}.
- **The claw recordings are not all separate flies.** The {{json:results/E140_aa_claw_soma_step/classes.json:i.flies_n ~ w}} claw recordings come from {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/flies_entries.csv:stage=kc_claw;cohort=wild_type;recording=mean;odor=aa:n_recordings ~ w}} flies: the recording at claws and somata on the same trials is a second recording of one of them. The first class missed by one of these recordings.
- **The fits are read on fewer wirings.** The fits of the megamat stages are read on the {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/models_entries.csv:group=A1;condition=on;stage=kc_soma;reading=curve_noise;wiring=common;odor=aa:n_recordings ~ w}} wirings that they were not fitted on, and the family and the imposed dendrites on {{E140_aa_claw_soma_step/models_entries.csv:group=family_held;condition=on;stage=kc_soma;reading=curve_noise;wiring=common;odor=aa:n_recordings ~ w}}.

### The megamat stage error through the flies' sampling {#fly-sampling}

**The question was how far the classes above depend on which flies were recorded.** Every bar and spread of the sections on the fit, on the normalization and on the inhibition among KCs is taken over the model's wirings ([the fit](#stage-fit), [the normalization](#normalization-ladder), [the inhibition among KCs](#kckc-rise)). The flies entered each class through one split of each stage's flies into a fitted half and a held-out half, so no class had an interval over the flies. The experiment is the megamat stage error through the flies' sampling. It ran no model.

**It asked four things of the saved runs.**

- Whether each megamat stage, and the megamat stage error, orders the saved models alike on every split of its flies.
- Whether megamat's claws and somata order models that differ only in the calyx.
- Whether each class since the fit of the megamat stages stands when the flies are resampled.
- Whether the panels with more flies show how many flies a stage needs.

**The design,** fixed before any statistic was read.

- **The settings.** {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:n_settings ~ .0f}} saved settings were read at all four megamat stages, on strength by odor: the unrefitted family, the fits of the megamat stages ([above](#stage-fit)), the settings along the normalization ([above](#normalization-ladder)), the doses and forms of the inhibition among KCs ([above](#kckc-rise)), and four models whose PN stage was set from the recorded dendrites ([above](#antennal-lobe-dendrites)). A setting's value is the mean over the held-out wirings.
- **Every split of the flies.** Each stage's flies are divided in two in every way that gives halves of the fit's sizes. For the megamat stage error, {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.megamat_stage_error.n_splits ~ .0f}} joint splits of the four stages are drawn.
- **Consistency** is the ρ, over the settings, between their errors against one half of the flies and against the other. It is 1 when both halves put the settings in the same order. A stage "tells the settings apart" if its consistency is {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/lock.json:definitions.thresholds.tells_median}} or more at the median split and {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/lock.json:definitions.thresholds.tells_min}} or more on every split.
- **Resampled flies.** Each stage's and each panel's flies are drawn anew with replacement, {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals.json:n_boot ~ .0f}} times, megamat's four stages together. A change's interval is the 5th to 95th percentile over the draws. Every class is computed again on each draw, with the wirings' spreads and bars as scored, and it "stands" if the class as scored is read on {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/lock.json:definitions.thresholds.class_share}} or more of the draws.
- **Each model is read once,** against all of a stage's flies. A half or a draw changes only the flies' mean view that the model is compared with, and the one-fly units stay those of all the flies. Read this way, the family's and the fitted model's megamat stage errors against the held-out half are within {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/check.json:values.family.diff ~ .3f}} and {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/check.json:values.E136:A1.diff ~ .3f}} of the fit's own scores, and {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/check.json:reclass_on_e136_split.reproduced ~ .0f}} of the {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/check.json:reclass_on_e136_split.n ~ .0f}} classes come out as scored.

How alike two halves of a stage's flies order the saved settings, on strength by odor: the consistency at the median split and at the split where it is least. The last two columns leave out the four models whose PN stage was set from the recorded dendrites. "Without aa's entry" takes aa's term out of the somata's squared error ([above](#claw-soma-aa)).

| | | | all settings {span=2} | without the four models set<br>from the recorded dendrites {span=2} |
| megamat stage | flies | splits | median split | least split | median split | least split |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PN dendrites | {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=pn_dendrite:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_dendrite.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_dendrite.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_dendrite.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_dendrite.median_without_e135 ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_dendrite.min_without_e135 ~ .2f}} |
| PN boutons | {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=pn_bouton:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_bouton.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_bouton.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_bouton.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_bouton.median_without_e135 ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.pn_bouton.min_without_e135 ~ .2f}} |
| KC claws | {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=kc_claw:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.kc_claw.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.kc_claw.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.kc_claw.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.kc_claw.median_without_e135 ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.kc_claw.min_without_e135 ~ .2f}} |

| KC somata, without aa's entry | {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=kc_soma:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits_no_aa.json:stages.kc_soma.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits_no_aa.json:stages.kc_soma.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits_no_aa.json:stages.kc_soma.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits_no_aa.json:stages.kc_soma.median_without_e135 ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits_no_aa.json:stages.kc_soma.min_without_e135 ~ .2f}} |
| megamat stage error: PN dendrites to KC somata | — | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.megamat_stage_error.n_splits ~ .0f}} joint | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.megamat_stage_error.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.megamat_stage_error.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.megamat_stage_error.median_without_e135 ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.megamat_stage_error.min_without_e135 ~ .2f}} |

**The megamat stage error orders the saved settings alike on every split of the flies, and no single stage does.** As a whole it passes: its consistency is above the first line at the median split and above the second at its least split. Each stage by itself falls short at the median split already, the claws furthest.

**The stages before the calyx fall short through four settings only.** The four models of the antennal lobe held to the recorded PN dendrites took their PN stage from all of the dendrite flies. A split of those flies shows each half a different part of what they were set from, and re-orders them. Without them the dendrites and the boutons tell the settings apart, and the claws and the somata fall further.

**So the megamat stage error passes because the stages before the calyx carry it.** The settings differ much more at the dendrites and the boutons than at the claws and the somata (the fitted model's changes are in the fourth table of this section). In practice it measures the stages before the calyx, and changes of the calyx large enough to move the boutons.

The same among settings that share one PN stage and differ only in the calyx: the {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/lock.json:len(definitions.calyx_groups.inhadd1000) ~ w}} at the weaker normalization, the fitted model with the doses and forms set beside it. The last column is, over the pairs of those settings, the share of the splits on which both halves agree which of the two is nearer the flies. One half is chance.

| megamat stage | splits | consistency,<br>median split | consistency,<br>least split | pairs of settings: share of the splits<br>on which the halves agree |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| PN boutons | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.pn_bouton/inhadd1000.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.pn_bouton/inhadd1000.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.pn_bouton/inhadd1000.min ~ .2f}} | — |
| KC claws | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_claw/inhadd1000.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_claw/inhadd1000.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_claw/inhadd1000.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_claw/inhadd1000.share_pairs_agree_mean ~ .2f}} |
| KC somata | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.share_pairs_agree_mean ~ .2f}} |
| KC somata, without aa's entry | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx_no_aa.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.n_splits ~ .0f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx_no_aa.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.median ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx_no_aa.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.min ~ .2f}} | {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx_no_aa.json:groups.kc_soma/inhadd1000.share_pairs_agree_mean ~ .2f}} |

**Megamat's claws and somata do not order the forms of the calyx.** At the somata the two halves agree on which of two such settings is nearer the flies on about half of the splits, which is chance. The claws come nearer: just under the line at the median split, and well under the second line on one split.

**So megamat's changes at the claws and the somata among these settings are descriptions.** They are the settings between which the sections on the normalization and on the inhibition among KCs chose. What can decide between forms of the calyx is the held-out panels.

**At the family's own normalization the somata do agree, for a reason that does not carry.** Among the {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/lock.json:len(definitions.calyx_groups.family_norm) ~ w}} settings there the consistency is {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/calyx.json:groups.kc_soma/family_norm.median ~ .2f}} at the median split. It rests on one setting, the fit without the inhibition among KCs, which is far worse than the rest. That is no reading of small differences in the calyx.

**Without aa's entry the somata nearly pass.** They come close to telling the settings apart, and they begin to order the forms of the calyx (the rows "without aa's entry"). aa is the odor that the flies lift between the claws and the somata and the model does not ([above](#claw-soma-aa)). A single odor's entry is a lead. Of the classes in the next table, one share changes without it: that of the family's calyx at the weaker normalization, from {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E139_normalization_ladder;arm=F_inhadd1000:share_scored ~ .2f}} to {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares_no_aa.csv:table=E139_normalization_ladder;arm=F_inhadd1000:share_scored ~ .2f}}.

The classes with the flies resampled. A change is of the megamat stage error on strength by odor, from the unrefitted family, the mean over the held-out wirings; negative is nearer the flies. "As scored" is against the held-out half of the flies, and the interval is over the draws of all the flies. "Both halves" is the share of the joint splits on which the fall is beyond its bar against each half.

All settings but the fitted model stand at the additive term {{json:results/E141_kckc_rise_forms/classes.json:base_point.rung ~ .0f}}, and the last four carry the fit's scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons. The last column is the share of the draws that give the class as scored.

| | megamat stage error, change from the family {span=4} | class {span=2} |

**The fitted model's fall on megamat stands through the flies.** Against all the flies its megamat stage error is below the family's, the interval over the resampled flies stays clear of zero, and the fall is beyond its bar against both halves on every joint split. The falls of the other settings at the weaker normalization stand as well.

**Its class does not.** "Holds" is read on about half of the draws. On the others a held-out panel or a megamat stage is worse than the family's spread. So the fitted model is nearer the flies on megamat whichever flies are drawn, and whether it keeps the held-out panels is not settled by these flies.

What the fitted model's class turns on: its change from the unrefitted family at each megamat stage and at the KC somata of each held-out panel of the test, on strength by odor in one-fly units; negative is nearer the flies. "As scored" is the mean over the held-out wirings, megamat against the held-out half of the flies. The last two columns are shares of the draws: those on which the change is a fall beyond its bar, and those on which it is a rise beyond the family's spread.

**The class turns on four readings that sit near their lines.** They are the claws' fall, the control mixtures with their {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=mixtures/cmix/on;half=2:n_flies ~ w}} flies, the APL16 panel's somata with APL on, and the other views of that panel's somata with APL off. None is clear of its bar or of the family's spread on every draw. The falls at the dendrites and the boutons, and of the megamat stage error, are clear on nearly every one.

**The classes that rest on a narrow margin change with the flies.** The holds at the two ends of the range of doses are read on fewer draws than the fitted model's. At {{E141/writeup_values.csv:arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;stage=four_stages;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=kckc_factor:value ~ .1f}} times the family's scale the APL16 panel's somata with APL on are worse than their spread on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals_changes.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms;arm=D1.8_inhadd1000;statistic=panel/apl16/apl16/on:share_worse ~ .2f}} of the draws. The scale of the inhibition among KCs alone and the rise with APL on only are megamat only on about half. Each of these classes is a near-tie, and the ladder of doses is read as a plateau without settled edges.

**Two verdicts on the inhibition among KCs do stand.** No dose does more than the fitted model's on {{min:E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms:does_more_than_a1_share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws or more. No other form holds in place of the matrix on {{min:E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E141_kckc_rise_forms:stands_in_share_scored ~ .2f}} or more. The class of the bouton scale alone, megamat only, stands as well.

How alike two halves of a panel's flies order the settings, by the number of flies to a half: the consistency over the settings read on each panel, at the KC somata on strength by odor, the median over {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/lock.json:seeds.n_count_draws ~ .0f}} random pairs of disjoint halves. The kiwi mixtures are read as uploaded, which describes the panel and gives no verdict on a model ([caveats](#caveats)).

| panel | flies | 2 to a half | 3 to a half | 4 to a half | 5 to a half |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| APL16 panel, APL on | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/on;half=2:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/on;half=2:median ~ .2f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/on;half=3:median ~ .2f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/on;half=4:median ~ .2f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/on;half=5:median ~ .2f}} |
| APL16 panel, APL off | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/off;half=2:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/off;half=2:median ~ .2f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/off;half=3:median ~ .2f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=apl16/apl16/off;half=4:median ~ .2f}} | — |
| kiwi mixtures | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=mixtures/kmix/on;half=2:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=mixtures/kmix/on;half=2:median ~ .2f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=mixtures/kmix/on;half=3:median ~ .2f}} | — | — |
| validation2 | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=validation2/validation2/on;half=2:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=validation2/validation2/on;half=2:median ~ .2f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=validation2/validation2/on;half=3:median ~ .2f}} | — | — |
| control mixtures | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=mixtures/cmix/on;half=2:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=mixtures/cmix/on;half=2:median ~ .2f}} | — | — | — |
| megamat | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=kc_soma/megamat/on;half=2:n_flies ~ .0f}} | {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/count_consistency.csv:key=kc_soma/megamat/on;half=2:median ~ .2f}} | — | — | — |

**More flies help on two panels and not on a third.** On the APL16 panel with APL off and on validation2 the consistency rises with the size of a half and passes the line at three flies to a half. On the APL16 panel with APL on it stays where it is from two recordings to a half to five. There the settings' small differences limit the order, and not the number of flies.

**So megamat's {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=kc_soma:n_flies ~ w}} soma flies are not simply too few.** The kiwi mixtures order the settings alike at two flies to a half already, and the control mixtures do not. Neither the number of flies nor the panel sets the consistency by itself. For megamat's somata, aa's step may matter as much as more flies.

**A fifth soma fly, in no fit and no class, reads as the four do.** It is the fly recorded at claws and somata on the same trials. Against the four soma flies it reads {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/fifth_fly.json:kc_soma/strength.fifth_against_all ~ .2f}} on strength by odor, inside their own range of {{E134_population_baseline/flies_views.csv:population=kc_soma__megamat__wild_type;name=strength:loo_min ~ .2f}} to {{E134_population_baseline/flies_views.csv:population=kc_soma__megamat__wild_type;name=strength:loo_max ~ .2f}}. The settings keep their order against it, at a ρ of {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/fifth_fly.json:kc_soma/strength.order_fifth_vs_four ~ .2f}} with their order against the four. The family reads {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/fifth_fly_settings.csv:stage=kc_soma;views=strength;setting=family:against_fifth ~ .2f}} against it and {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/fifth_fly_settings.csv:stage=kc_soma;views=strength;setting=family:against_one_of_four_mean ~ .2f}} against a single one of the four, as a model near the flies' mean view should against one fly.

**A second recording of one fly repeats, and the flies differ.** The second claw recording of that fly reads {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/fifth_fly.json:kc_claw/strength.second_against_first ~ .2f}} against the first, where one claw fly against the others reads {{E134_population_baseline/flies_views.csv:population=kc_claw__megamat__wild_type;name=strength:loo_min ~ .2f}} to {{E134_population_baseline/flies_views.csv:population=kc_claw__megamat__wild_type;name=strength:loo_max ~ .2f}}. So what separates the claw flies is the flies, and not the noise of a session.

**Realism.** No model was run or changed, so no band was read anew. What would be hard to defend in the fitted model is as stated [above](#stage-fit). No gain is claimed here, so none has a cost.

**The one realism flag among the forms of the inhibition among KCs got an interval** ([above](#kckc-rise)). The shuffled matrix's response rate ρ with APL on, on one wiring, is {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals.json:realism_flag.rate_rho_on_all ~ .2f}} against the band's {{const:kcq/apl_effect.py:RATE_RHO_MIN ~ .2f}}, with an interval of {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals.json:realism_flag.lo ~ .2f}} to {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals.json:realism_flag.hi ~ .2f}} over the resampled recordings. It is under the band on {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/intervals.json:realism_flag.share_below_band ~ .2f}} of the draws: no accident of one sample of the flies, and not certain either.

**What follows.**

- **The megamat stage error stays the number that models are chosen and gated on.** It orders the saved settings alike whichever flies are drawn. What it reads is the stages before the calyx.
- **Forms of the calyx are judged on the held-out panels,** and megamat's changes at the claws and the somata are given as descriptions.
- **A class by a narrow margin is named a near-tie:** the fitted model's hold, the holds at both ends of the range of doses, and the classes of the scale of the inhibition among KCs alone, of the rise with APL on only and of the gain at the claws.
- **The second fit of the megamat stages has since been made,** on the other wirings and against the other halves of the flies. The fall repeats in every fit and the hold in none, and the fits end at different calyx factors, as megamat's claws and somata led one to expect ([below](#stage-fit-again)). How the calyx is to be constrained is a decision for those who direct the project ([pending](#pending)).

**Limits.**

- **The model is read once against all the flies.** Against the held-out half this is within the tolerance set beforehand, {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/check.json:tol}}, of the fit's own reading. Single stages are less exact, by up to {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/check.json:largest_abs_diff_any ~ .2f}}. One class differs: the fit's bouton scale alone at the smaller term reads strength at another view's cost in place of megamat only.
- **The resampled flies are the recorded flies.** A draw of {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=kc_soma:n_flies ~ w}} soma flies cannot show what one more would add. The intervals say how much the recorded flies move a number, and not what other flies would give.
- **The claws and the somata have {{json:results/E143_megamat_fly_sampling/splits.json:stages.kc_soma.n_splits ~ w}} splits each,** so their least values are single splits.
- **Only the held-out wirings are read,** and the wirings' spreads and bars are kept as scored.
- **The lines are conventions:** the two lines for consistency and the share of draws for a class were fixed beforehand, and a class just under a line is not a different finding from one just over it.
- **Validation2 is held out here.** Nothing was chosen from the regression that was read on its odors ([caveats](#caveats)).
- **Concentrations.** Every number of the APL16 panel involves paa, euc and -aPine ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)).

### The step from the PN dendrites to the PN boutons by itself, with the recorded dendrites under the stage fit's calyx {#dendrite-bouton-step}

**The question was what the largest stage error left under the fitted model is made of.** The PN boutons are the megamat stage at which the fitted model stays furthest from what the fitted half of the flies predicts for the other half: {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength_mean:value ~ .3f}} against {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=yardstick_half_to_half;stage=pn_bouton;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=strength:value ~ .3f}} ([above](#stage-fit)). The family does not explain the step from the dendrites to the boutons: its boutons are level with the dendrite recordings taken as the prediction ([the stage table](#stage-table)). And the recorded dendrites had been tried under the family's calyx only ([above](#antennal-lobe-dendrites)).

**The experiment is the step from the PN dendrites to the PN boutons by itself, with the recorded dendrites under the stage fit's calyx.** It asked five things.

- Which odors carry the step in the flies, and whether any saved model makes it.
- Whether the connectomes' counts of boutons make it.
- Whether it is a matter of how the bouton regions are counted.
- Whether APL's inhibition of the boutons hides it.
- Whether the recorded dendrites, put under the fitted model's calyx, bring the stages after them nearer the flies.

**The design.**

- **A first step with no run.** It reads the flies' step odor by odor, on all the flies and on the two halves of the fit, and sets every saved model's step beside it. It reads the recorded dendrites with each glomerulus counted by its boutons in a connectome, as the prediction for the bouton flies. And it reads the boutons with every group of like-tuned regions counted once.
- **The runs put the recorded dendrites in the PN stage's place,** "imposed" as before ([above](#antennal-lobe-dendrites)). On the {{json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/lock.json:len(glomeruli)}} glomeruli that the recordings and the model's input share, the PNs' rates take the flies' arrangement, rank for rank on the stage's own rates. The scales of the calyx are fixed, nothing is fitted, and both response rates are held again on every wiring.
- **The main arm stands on the fitted model's own PN stage.** It has the fitted model's normalization and both of its calyx scales, and the dendrites are ranked onto the fitted model's own PN rates. So it differs from the fitted model only in how those rates are arranged. The arm planned first, on the family's PN rates, could not be held (below), and the main arm was moved before any run was scored.
- **Two controls and one held-out arm.** The controls permute the glomerulus labels and the odor labels of the imposed table. One arm on the APL16 input, with its twelve megamat odors set, gives the realism bands and the APL effect.
- **"The dendrites carry"** was fixed beforehand. The root mean square of the boutons', the claws' and the somata's errors has to be below the fitted model's by more than {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:classes.dendrites_carry.se_multiple ~ w}} standard errors over the held-out wirings. No stage may be worse than the fitted model's by more than the family's spread, and every stage has to be below both permuted controls.
- **"APL at the boutons hides the step"** is read on the family's PN stage. With APL's bouton scale at the fit's value or at none, the imposed dendrites' boutons have to be below the no-step yardstick on {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:classes.apl_hides_step.min_wirings ~ w}} of the {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/lock.json:classes.apl_hides_step.n_wirings ~ w}} wirings, and not so with the family's scale.
- **An imposed arm is a bound, not a model.** Its dendrites are the flies' own, set from all the dendrite flies. It shows the most that a step from the ORNs to the PN dendrites could reach on the recorded glomeruli.

Strength by odor at the megamat stages with the recorded dendrites imposed, in one-fly units; lower is better. A value is the mean over the {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:classes.dendrites_carry.downstream.n ~ w}} held-out wirings, against the held-out half of each stage's flies, so it is compared with the values of the fit ([above](#stage-fit)) and not with the stage table. "After the dendrites" is the root mean square of the boutons', the claws' and the somata's errors. A change is from the fitted model on the same wirings, with its standard error over them.
