An imposed arm's dendrites are the flies' own, so its value at the dendrites and a megamat stage error that includes it are not a model's values.

**The outcome: under the fitted model's calyx the recorded dendrites do not carry.** The dendrites come nearer the flies, and the three stages after them together move away by {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:classes.dendrites_carry.downstream.change ~ +.3f}}, with a standard error of {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:classes.dendrites_carry.downstream.se ~ .3f}}. The boutons and the claws each rise by many times the family's spread at that stage. The somata's change is inside their spread, where under the family's calyx the imposed dendrites had moved them away as well ([above](#antennal-lobe-dendrites)).

**The recorded arrangement is still better than a permuted one.** The arm is below both permuted controls at every stage, so the runs read the dendrites' structure. It is worse than the arrangement that the fitted model's own PN stage makes.

**So a step from the ORNs to the PN dendrites cannot bring the later stages nearer the flies through the recorded glomeruli.** The recorded dendrites are the most that a term at that step, held to those glomeruli, can reach. With them the dendrites are nearer the flies, and the boutons and the claws are further away than the fitted model has them.

**That bounds the lead of a step with terms per glomerulus** ([pending](#pending)). Such a step could be judged on the dendrites as a stage of its own, and it would not lower the stages after them. The bound does not cover the {{diff:json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/timing_step1.json:size.n_input -- json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/lock.json:len(glomeruli) ~ w}} input glomeruli that the dendrite recordings do not hold.

**With the dendrites imposed the fitted model loses its standing against the no-step yardstick at the boutons.** Against the held-out half of the bouton flies that yardstick reads {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.boutons.no_step ~ .3f}}. The fitted model's boutons are below it on {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.boutons.variants.A1.n_below_all ~ .0f}} of the {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.boutons.variants.A1.n_wirings ~ .0f}} wirings, and with the dendrites imposed they are below it on {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.boutons.variants.E142_a1stage_imposed.n_below_all ~ .0f}}.

**With the flies resampled the verdict stands.** Each stage's flies are drawn anew {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.n_boot ~ ,.0f}} times, as in the megamat stage error through the flies' sampling ([above](#fly-sampling)). Against all the flies the megamat stage error rises from the fitted model's by {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.changes.stage.all_flies ~ +.3f}}, and by {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.changes.stage.lo ~ +.3f}} to {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.changes.stage.hi ~ +.3f}} over the draws. The rises at the boutons and at the claws stay clear of zero as well, at {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.changes.pn_bouton.lo ~ +.3f}} to {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.changes.pn_bouton.hi ~ +.3f}} and {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.changes.kc_claw.lo ~ +.3f}} to {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.changes.kc_claw.hi ~ +.3f}}. The class "the dendrites carry" is read on {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:beside.e143.share_holds ~ .0%}} of the draws.

The response-rate part of strength by odor at each megamat stage, split as for the fit ([above](#stage-fit)). "Spread" is the standard deviation of the model's response rates over the odors as a multiple of the flies', and "order" is the error that the spread does not explain, in one-fly units. Here each is the mean over the held-out wirings of the wiring's own split, against the held-out half of the flies. The fit's table splits the wirings' mean view, so the fitted model's values differ a little between the two tables.

| | PN dendrites {span=2} | PN boutons {span=2} | KC claws {span=2} | KC somata {span=2} |
| model | spread | order | spread | order | spread | order | spread | order |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| the fitted model | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_dendrite_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_dendrite_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_bouton_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_bouton_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_claw_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_claw_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_soma_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_soma_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} |
| the recorded dendrites imposed on its PN stage | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_dendrite_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_dendrite_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_bouton_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_bouton_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_claw_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_claw_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_soma_split_strength_rate_sd_ratio ~ .2f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:kc_soma_split_strength_rate_order ~ .2f}} |

**The boutons get the flies' spread and keep the dendrites' order.** With the dendrites imposed, the boutons' response rates spread over the odors as widely as the flies' do. Their correlation with the flies' boutons over the odors stays where it was, at {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_bouton_split_strength_rate_r ~ .2f}} against the fitted model's {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=A1;half=held;wiring=mean_T:pn_bouton_split_strength_rate_r ~ .2f}}. The flies re-order the odors between the dendrites and the boutons, and the model's step does not. So the flies' spread on the dendrites' order costs more than the narrow spread did, and the order part grows.

**At the claws the spread overshoots, and at the somata the two parts cancel.** The claws' response rates spread more widely than the flies', with a larger order part. The somata reach the flies' spread, their order part grows, and their error hardly changes.

**The flies' step re-orders the odors, and no saved model's step follows it.** From the dendrites to the boutons IaA and pa rise and t2h, 1-6ol, benz and va fall, in the response rate and in the mean response, on both halves of the flies. In none of the {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/lock.json:len(model.groups) ~ w}} saved models and controls does the step by odor follow the flies': over the {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/lock.json:len(odors) ~ .0f}} odors the model's change and the flies' correlate at {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/models_step.csv:step=pn_dendrite->pn_bouton;row=common;part=rate:r ~ +.2f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/models_step.csv:step=pn_dendrite->pn_bouton;row=common;part=rate:r ~ +.2f}} on the response rate.

**From the boutons to the claws the flies undo part of the step.** IaA, eb, ep and benz fall, and ms and aa rise, on both halves.

**The connectomes' counts of boutons do not make the flies' step.** A dendrite recording counts each glomerulus once, and a bouton recording counts it by its regions. Taken as the prediction for the bouton flies, the recorded dendrites read {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/yardsticks.csv:stage=pn_bouton;glomeruli=dendrites;calyx=hemibrain-first;fly_set=all:no_step ~ .3f}} on strength by odor (lower is better), and {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/yardsticks.csv:stage=pn_bouton;glomeruli=dendrites;calyx=hemibrain-first;fly_set=all:counts ~ .3f}} with each glomerulus counted by its boutons in the hemibrain. Against the two halves of the bouton flies that gain is {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/yardsticks.csv:stage=pn_bouton;glomeruli=dendrites;calyx=hemibrain-first;fly_set=fit:below_no_step_by ~ .3f}} and {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/yardsticks.csv:stage=pn_bouton;glomeruli=dendrites;calyx=hemibrain-first;fly_set=held:below_no_step_by ~ .3f}}, where {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/lock.json:classes.counts_make_step.margin ~ .2f}} was asked. Of {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/lock.json:classes.counts_make_step.n_perm ~ ,.0f}} permutations of the counts among the glomeruli, {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/yardsticks.csv:stage=pn_bouton;glomeruli=dendrites;calyx=hemibrain-first;fly_set=all:null_percentile_of_actual ~ .1f}} % read lower still, where fewer than {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/lock.json:classes.counts_make_step.null_percentile ~ .0f}} % were asked.

**The male CNS's counts do worse.** With them the dendrites read {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/yardsticks.csv:stage=pn_bouton;glomeruli=dendrites;calyx=malecns-left;fly_set=all:counts ~ .3f}} and {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/yardsticks.csv:stage=pn_bouton;glomeruli=dendrites;calyx=malecns-right;fly_set=all:counts ~ .3f}} for its two calyces, above the reading with each glomerulus counted once. The family's own boutons on the two male calyces stand in the same order ([below](#male-calyces)).

**Nor is it a matter of how the regions are counted.** The model's bouton reading counts each glomerulus by its hemibrain boutons already. With every group of like-tuned bouton regions counted once in the flies ([above](#before-calyx)), the fitted model's change at the boutons is {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/classes_step0.json:readings.change ~ +.3f}} against a spread of {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/classes_step0.json:readings.spread ~ .3f}}. All {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/classes_step0.json:len(reclasses.equal_weight) ~ w}} classes of the fits and of the settings along the normalization that were read again are as scored, and they are so with the boutons left out of the megamat stage error as well.

**So two outcomes are ruled out:** that the boutons' error lies in how the model's boutons are read, and that the fitted model's gain there depends on how the flies' regions are counted.

**APL's inhibition of the boutons does not hide the step.** On the family's PN stage, with the fit's scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons and the family's inhibition among KCs, the imposed dendrites' boutons read {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_imposed_aplpn;half=held;wiring!=mean_S;wiring!=mean_T;wiring!=common:pn_bouton ~ .2f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_megamat.csv:variant=E142_imposed_aplpn;half=held;wiring!=mean_S;wiring!=mean_T;wiring!=common:pn_bouton ~ .2f}} over the eight wirings. They are below the no-step yardstick on {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_classes.json:classes.apl_hides_step.arms.E142_imposed_aplpn.n_below ~ .0f}} of them, as with the family's bouton scale.

**The arm without APL's inhibition of the boutons could not be held.** With the APL scale at the top of its range, the response rate with APL on stayed at {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_aplpn_none:rate_on_max ~ .3f}} against the flies' {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_aplpn_none:target_on_max ~ .4f}}, on the one wiring read. The arm is left out, so the verdict rests on the arm at the fit's scale.

**The fit's larger inhibition among KCs cannot be held on the family's PN rates.** With the dendrites imposed on the family's PN stage and both of the fit's calyx scales, the KCs' threshold ended at the bottom of its range, {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:thr_factor_max ~ .1f}} of each wiring's fitted threshold, on {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:n_at_bound_thr ~ .0f}} of the {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:n_wirings ~ .0f}} wirings. The response rate with APL off stayed at {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:rate_off_min ~ .3f}} to {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:rate_off_max ~ .3f}}, against targets of {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:target_off_min ~ .3f}} to {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:target_off_max ~ .3f}}. With the fit's bouton scale alone both rates are held, and with APL off APL's scales play no part. So it is the larger inhibition among KCs that cannot be held there.

**The fit had met this already.** With its additive term returned alone to the family's value, the response rates could no longer be held ([above](#stage-fit)). So the larger inhibition among KCs can be held at megamat's operating point only beside the weaker normalization. A design that changes the fitted model's PN stage has to keep its normalization with its calyx, as the main arm does.

**No ORN input returns the recorded dendrites through the family's antennal lobe.** The runs set the PN stage's rates directly from the recordings, as the earlier runs with the recorded dendrites did. The other way is an ORN input solved so that the model's own PN stage returns the imposed table. That solve misses the {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/routes_check.json:check1.tol_hz ~ .1f}} Hz asked on {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/routes_check.json:check1.n_failing ~ .0f}} of the {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/routes_check.json:check1.n_tables ~ .0f}} tables that the runs need, by {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/routes_check_tables.csv:wiring=real;panel=megamat;table=imposed:max_abs_diff_hz ~ .1f}} Hz on the megamat table. For {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/routes_check_tables.csv:wiring=real;panel=megamat;table=imposed:n_odors_without_root ~ w}} of that table's {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/routes_check_tables.csv:wiring=real;panel=megamat;table=imposed:n_set_odors ~ .0f}} odors no input returns the recorded rates: an input that drives those PNs up drives the antennal lobe's inhibition up more.

The main arm on the APL16 input, a test on recordings that no fit of the stages saw. The somata's strength errors are means over the eight wirings, in one-fly units; lower is better. The APL-effect statistics are read through the calibrated readout, each wiring scored by itself ([how the statistics are defined](#fn-apl-effect)). The family was fitted to these recordings, so its row is a fit.

| | KC somata of the APL16 panel, strength by odor {span=2} | APL effect, lowest to highest wiring {span=2} |
| model | APL on | APL off | pattern part | mean APL effect |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| the flies: floor or value | floor {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=apl16;condition=on:strength_floor ~ .2f}} | floor {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=apl16;condition=off:strength_floor ~ .2f}} | floor {{data:apl_effect_pattern_floor ~ .3f}} | {{data:apl_effect_mean ~ +.3f}} |
| the family, not refitted | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_panels.csv:arm=family;panel=apl16;condition=on;wiring=mean:view_strength ~ .3f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_panels.csv:arm=family;panel=apl16;condition=off;wiring=mean:view_strength ~ .3f}} | {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=family;wiring!=mean:cal_pattern ~ .3f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=family;wiring!=mean:cal_pattern ~ .3f}} | {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=family;wiring!=mean:cal_mean ~ +.3f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=family;wiring!=mean:cal_mean ~ +.3f}} |
| the fitted model | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_panels.csv:arm=A1;panel=apl16;condition=on;wiring=mean:view_strength ~ .3f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_panels.csv:arm=A1;panel=apl16;condition=off;wiring=mean:view_strength ~ .3f}} | {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=A1;wiring!=mean:cal_pattern ~ .3f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=A1;wiring!=mean:cal_pattern ~ .3f}} | {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=A1;wiring!=mean:cal_mean ~ +.3f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=A1;wiring!=mean:cal_mean ~ +.3f}} |
| the recorded dendrites imposed on the fitted model's PN stage | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_panels.csv:arm=E142_a1stage_imposed;panel=apl16;condition=on;wiring=mean:view_strength ~ .3f}} | {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_panels.csv:arm=E142_a1stage_imposed;panel=apl16;condition=off;wiring=mean:view_strength ~ .3f}} | {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:cal_pattern ~ .3f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:cal_pattern ~ .3f}} | {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:cal_mean ~ +.3f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:cal_mean ~ +.3f}} |

**On the APL16 panel the imposed dendrites change little.** The somata's strength errors stay close to the fitted model's, with APL on and with APL off. The pattern part of the APL-effect loss is level with the fitted model's and above the family's, and the mean APL effect falls further below the flies'. These rows describe a held-out test, and they gate nothing.

**Realism.** On the APL16 input the main arm stays inside all four realism bands on all eight wirings, and no arm is flagged. Both response rates are held on every wiring, so the bands on the response rate and on the ratio hold by construction.

- **The other two bands.** The response rate ρ with APL on is {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:rate_rho_on ~ .2f}} on the lowest wiring, against the band's {{const:kcq/apl_effect.py:RATE_RHO_MIN ~ .2f}}. The numbers of odors per KC are at most {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:opk_on_tv ~ .3f}} from the flies' distribution with APL on and {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:opk_off_tv ~ .3f}} with APL off, against bands of {{data:noise_ctrl_odors_per_kc_tv_max ~ .3f}} and {{data:noise_tnt_odors_per_kc_tv_max ~ .3f}}.
- **The largest spike counts are near the fitted model's.** On the APL16 input a KC's largest count reaches {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:max_count_on ~ .0f}} with APL on and {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=E142_a1stage_imposed;wiring!=mean:max_count_off ~ .0f}} with APL off, against the fitted model's {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=A1;wiring!=mean:max_count_on ~ .0f}} and {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/score_step1_apl16.csv:variant=A1;wiring!=mean:max_count_off ~ .0f}}.
- **On megamat the main arm is held at the flies' response rate,** {{E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_holds.csv:panel=megamat;arm=a1stage_imposed:target_on_max ~ .4f}} with APL on, with no parameter at an end of its range. Its KC thresholds are {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=a1stage_imposed:fixed_thr ~ .1f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=a1stage_imposed:fixed_thr ~ .1f}}, and its largest spike counts with APL on {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=a1stage_imposed:max_count_on ~ .0f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=a1stage_imposed:max_count_on ~ .0f}}.
- **The hold that failed shows what a missed target looks like.** There the thresholds are {{min:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:fixed_thr ~ .1f}} to {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:fixed_thr ~ .1f}}, and the largest spike counts with APL on reach {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=imposed_a1:max_count_on ~ .0f}}. Its megamat somata read lower than the fitted model's, and that is not a gain: the run is not at megamat's operating point and has no class.
- **What would be hard to defend** in the fitted model is as stated [above](#stage-fit). This experiment adds that its larger inhibition among KCs is tied to its weak normalization. No gain is claimed here, so none has a cost.

**Limits.**

- **An imposed arm is a bound.** Its dendrites are set from all the dendrite flies, the held-out half among them. So its value at the dendrites and a megamat stage error that includes it are not a model's values.
- **Strength by odor only.** Sharing between odors and breadth among units were not read for these runs.
- **The imposed table covers {{json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/lock.json:len(glomeruli)}} of the model's {{json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/timing_step1.json:size.n_input}} input glomeruli** and megamat's {{json:results/E142_dendrite_bouton_step/lock.json:len(odors) ~ .0f}} odors. The other input glomeruli keep the stage's own rates, and {{max:E142_dendrite_bouton_step/step1_variants.csv:panel=megamat;arm=a1stage_imposed:clip_n_clipped ~ w}} of the table's entries are clipped to the PN stage's range.
- **The verdict on APL's inhibition of the boutons rests on one arm,** at the fit's scale. The arm without that inhibition could not be held, and another operating point was not tried.
- **The solved input was checked at the family's normalization only.** Whether one exists at the fitted model's was not read.
- **The flies are few after the dendrites.** Megamat's claws and somata do not order forms of the calyx that differ little ([above](#fly-sampling)). The rises here at the boutons and at the claws are far larger and stand with the flies resampled, and the somata's change does not.
- **One mapped brain carries the runs.** All eight wirings are the hemibrain's, and the male CNS enters only through its counts of boutons in the first step.
- **Validation2 was not read.**
- **Concentrations.** Every number of the APL16 panel involves paa, euc and -aPine ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)).

**What follows.**

- **The step from the ORNs to the PN dendrites is closed as a way to the later stages,** at its bound on the recorded glomeruli ([pending](#pending)).
- **The boutons' error is not made before the dendrites.** It lies at the step from the dendrites to the boutons, in which regions a bouton recording holds, or in what two sets of recordings differ by. The second cannot be read while the regions carry no labels, and the third was sized next ([below](#bouton-step-forms)).
- **A form at that step that re-orders the odors as the flies do was looked for next, and none was found** ([below](#bouton-step-forms)). The connectomes' counts, the counting of regions and APL's inhibition of the boutons are not that form, and neither is one weight per recorded glomerulus or a feature of the whole odor.
- **So whether the boutons keep an equal weight in the megamat stage error is a decision for those who direct the project** ([pending](#pending)).
- **The fitted model's PN stage and its calyx are changed together,** or not at all.

### What could make the flies' step from the PN dendrites to the PN boutons {#bouton-step-forms}

**The question was whether the flies' step from the dendrites to the boutons has a form that a model could be given.** The boutons are the megamat stage at which the fitted model stays furthest from the flies. From the dendrites to the boutons the flies re-order the odors, and no saved model does. The connectomes' counts of boutons, the counting of the flies' regions and APL's inhibition of the boutons had been ruled out as the source ([above](#dendrite-bouton-step)). What was left was a lead without a form.

**The experiment is what could make the flies' step from the PN dendrites to the PN boutons, and it ran no model.** It asked three things of the recordings.

- Whether one weight per recorded glomerulus makes the step.
- Whether a feature of the whole odor, added to the dendrites' entry, makes it.
- Whether the step is larger than what two sets of recordings differ by at one stage.

**The design,** fixed before any statistic was read.

- **Left-out odors judge a form.** A form is fitted on all of megamat's odors but one and predicts the one left out. The {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(odors) ~ .0f}} predictions make one view of the boutons' strength by odor. Its error against all the bouton flies, and against each half of them, is the left-out reading.
- **One weight per glomerulus.** Each of the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(glomeruli.dendrites) ~ .0f}} glomeruli of the dendrite recordings gets a weight, the same for every odor and every dendrite fly, where the connectomes' counts of boutons stood before ([above](#dendrite-bouton-step)). The weights are fitted to the bouton flies' strength by odor, with a penalty toward equal weights.
- **A feature of the whole odor.** The features were fixed beforehand, {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(constants.features) ~ w}} of them: the share of the odor's summed dendrite response in its {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.top_k ~ w}} strongest glomeruli, its summed response at the ORNs, the same share at the ORNs, and its entry in the other part of strength by odor. The boutons' entry is predicted as a linear function of the dendrites' entry and one feature.
- **"Makes the step"** asks what was asked of the connectomes' counts. The left-out reading has to be below the no-step yardstick by {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.margin ~ .2f}} against each half of the bouton flies. It also has to be below the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.null_percentile ~ .0f}}th percentile of {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.n_perm ~ ,.0f}} permutations: of the weights among the glomeruli, or of the features among the odors with the best feature taken each time.
- **A check of power came first.** Known weights were planted on the dendrite flies, each with the noise of a resampled set of the bouton flies: the hemibrain's counts of boutons, deals of each bouton fly's groups of like-tuned regions onto the glomeruli ([above](#before-calyx)), and draws as uneven as the fitted weights. The method has power if, for {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.power_share ~ .0%}} of the plants of each kind whose planted step reads {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.power_eligible ~ .1f}} or more, the left-out reading is below the plant's own no-step yardstick by the same margin. The strength of the penalty, among {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(constants.penalties) ~ w}}, was chosen by this check alone.
- **Two sets of recordings at one stage.** The megamat soma flies and the control flies of the APL16 panel were given {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:n_odors ~ w}} odors in common. Each set's mean strength by odor is read against the other's, in the other's one-fly units, with the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.sets_q.0 ~ .0f}}th to {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.sets_q.1 ~ .0f}}th percentile over {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.n_boot_sets ~ ,.0f}} resamples of the flies of both sets. The no-step yardstick at the boutons gets the same interval over the dendrite and the bouton flies. The step is "not told apart" if the intervals overlap.

The boutons' strength by odor with each form, read on left-out odors, in the bouton flies' one-fly units; lower is better. "No step" takes the dendrite recordings as the prediction. The halves are those of the fit of the megamat stages ([above](#stage-fit)), the fitted half first. The last two rows are the percentiles that a form has to be below.

| form | all the bouton flies | the two halves<br>of the bouton flies |
|---|---|---|
| no step | {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=all:no_step ~ .3f}} | {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=fit:no_step ~ .3f}} / {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=held:no_step ~ .3f}} |
| one weight per glomerulus | {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=all:error ~ .3f}} | {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=fit:error ~ .3f}} / {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=held:error ~ .3f}} |
| the dendrites' entry rescaled, with no feature | {{E146/features.csv:form=rescaled_alone;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} | {{E146/features.csv:form=rescaled_alone;fly_set=fit:left_out ~ .3f}} / {{E146/features.csv:form=rescaled_alone;fly_set=held:left_out ~ .3f}} |
| with the entry in the other part of strength by odor | {{E146/features.csv:form=other_part;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} | {{E146/features.csv:form=other_part;fly_set=fit:left_out ~ .3f}} / {{E146/features.csv:form=other_part;fly_set=held:left_out ~ .3f}} |
| with the dendrites' share in the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.top_k ~ w}} strongest glomeruli | {{E146/features.csv:form=dendrite_top3_share;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} | {{E146/features.csv:form=dendrite_top3_share;fly_set=fit:left_out ~ .3f}} / {{E146/features.csv:form=dendrite_top3_share;fly_set=held:left_out ~ .3f}} |
| with the summed response at the ORNs | {{E146/features.csv:form=orn_summed;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} | {{E146/features.csv:form=orn_summed;fly_set=fit:left_out ~ .3f}} / {{E146/features.csv:form=orn_summed;fly_set=held:left_out ~ .3f}} |
| with the ORNs' share in the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.top_k ~ w}} strongest glomeruli | {{E146/features.csv:form=orn_top3_share;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} | {{E146/features.csv:form=orn_top3_share;fly_set=fit:left_out ~ .3f}} / {{E146/features.csv:form=orn_top3_share;fly_set=held:left_out ~ .3f}} |
| the weights permuted among the glomeruli, {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.null_percentile ~ .0f}}th percentile | {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/classes.json:weight_makes_step.null_p05 ~ .3f}} | — |
| the features permuted among the odors, the best of them, {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.null_percentile ~ .0f}}th percentile | {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/classes.json:feature_makes_step.null_best_p05 ~ .3f}} | — |

**The outcome: neither class of form makes the step, and the step is not told apart from what two sets of recordings differ by.** The lead closes on the recorded glomeruli, and two decisions go to those who direct the project ([pending](#pending)).

**No weight per glomerulus makes the step on left-out odors.** The weights' margin below the no-step yardstick is {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=all:below_no_step_by ~ +.3f}} against all the bouton flies, and {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=fit:below_no_step_by ~ +.3f}} and {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=held:below_no_step_by ~ +.3f}} against the two halves, where {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.margin ~ .2f}} was asked. Of the permuted weights, {{E146/bound.csv:reading=left_out;fly_set=all:null_percentile_of_actual ~ .0f}} % read lower. The margin is met at none of the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(constants.penalties) ~ w}} strengths of the penalty: the left-out reading runs from {{E146/bound_by_penalty.csv:penalty=0.0;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} with no penalty to {{E146/bound_by_penalty.csv:penalty=3.0;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} with the strongest.

**The weights carry from fly to fly and not from odor to odor.** Fitted on all the odors to one half of the bouton flies, they read {{E146/bound.csv:reading=fit_half_all_odors_read_on_held;fly_set=held:error ~ .3f}} against the other half, where the no-step yardstick reads {{E146/bound.csv:reading=fit_half_all_odors_read_on_held;fly_set=held:no_step ~ .3f}}. Fitted on all the odors but one, they do not predict that odor. So a weighting describes the odors that it was fitted to, and it is not a rule that carries to a new odor.

**Every bouton fly shows the step.** Each of the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(recordings.pn_bouton.flies) ~ w}} bouton flies moves each of the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(constants.seven_odors) ~ w}} odors that carry the step in the direction of the flies' mean, in the response rate and in the mean response: IaA, pa and ep up, and t2h, 1-6ol, benz and va down. So the step belongs to the bouton flies as a set, and not to one fly.

**No feature of the whole odor makes the step.** The best of them, the odor's entry in the other part of strength by odor, reads {{E146/features.csv:form=other_part;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} against all the bouton flies. The dendrites' entry rescaled with no feature reads {{E146/features.csv:form=rescaled_alone;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}}. No feature is below the no-step yardstick by the margin on both halves, and none comes near the permuted features' percentile.

**The check of power holds, on one kind of plant.** At the chosen strength of the penalty the method finds {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=1.0;kind=deals:n_pass ~ .0f}} of the {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=1.0;kind=deals:n_eligible ~ .0f}} deals of the tuning groups whose planted step is large enough. The plants of the hemibrain's counts have a planted step of {{max:E146/power.csv:kind=hemibrain:no_step ~ .2f}} at most, too small to need finding. Of the {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=1.0;kind=uneven:n ~ .0f}} uneven draws, {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=1.0;kind=uneven:n_eligible ~ w}} have a step large enough there. With the dendrite flies resampled as well, {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading!=the plants;penalty=1.0;kind=deals:n_pass ~ .0f}} of {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading!=the plants;penalty=1.0;kind=deals:n_eligible ~ .0f}} deals are found, a share of {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading!=the plants;penalty=1.0;kind=deals:share ~ .2f}} where {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.power_share ~ .1f}} was asked.

**Very uneven weightings are covered by a reading beside the locked one.** With no penalty the check holds as well: the method finds {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=0.0;kind=uneven:n_pass ~ w}} of the {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=0.0;kind=uneven:n_eligible ~ w}} uneven draws and {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=0.0;kind=deals:n_pass ~ .0f}} of the {{E146/power_summary.csv:reading=the plants;penalty=0.0;kind=deals:n_eligible ~ .0f}} deals whose planted step is large enough. The flies' weights then read {{E146/bound_by_penalty.csv:penalty=0.0;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .3f}} on left-out odors, far above the no-step yardstick. So the verdict is firmest for weightings like the deals of the tuning groups. For very uneven ones it rests on a strength of the penalty that the locked rule did not choose.

Two sets of flies read at one stage, beside the step. A value is the first set's mean strength by odor read against the second's, in the second set's one-fly units; lower says that the sets are nearer each other. The interval is the {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.sets_q.0 ~ .0f}}th to {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:constants.sets_q.1 ~ .0f}}th percentile over resampled flies of both sets, and the floor is what a resampled set of the second set's own flies gives.

| comparison | flies, first set /<br>second set | odors | value | interval over<br>resampled flies | floor of the<br>second set |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| the megamat somata against the control flies' somata | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:n_scored ~ .0f}} / {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:n_reference ~ .0f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:n_odors ~ .0f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:measured ~ .2f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:lo ~ .2f}} to {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:hi ~ .2f}} | {{E146/identities.csv:name=control_floor:reproduced ~ .2f}} |
| the control flies' somata against the megamat somata | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=control_in_megamat_units:n_scored ~ .0f}} / {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=control_in_megamat_units:n_reference ~ .0f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=control_in_megamat_units:n_odors ~ .0f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=control_in_megamat_units:measured ~ .2f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=control_in_megamat_units:lo ~ .2f}} to {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=control_in_megamat_units:hi ~ .2f}} | {{E146/identities.csv:name=megamat_floor:reproduced ~ .2f}} |
| the step: the PN dendrites against the PN boutons (the no-step yardstick) | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=no_step:n_scored ~ .0f}} / {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=no_step:n_reference ~ .0f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=no_step:n_odors ~ .0f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=no_step:measured ~ .2f}} | {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=no_step:lo ~ .2f}} to {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=no_step:hi ~ .2f}} | {{E134_population_baseline/writeup_stage_table.csv:group=megamat_fitted;stage=pn_bouton:strength_floor ~ .2f}} |

**The step is not told apart from what two sets of recordings differ by.** The interval of the no-step yardstick overlaps both intervals between the sets. Its value is above both of theirs, and all three are far above the floors. This is a statement of size, and it names no cause.

**What that leaves open.** The megamat soma flies and the control flies were recorded by different experimenters, and so were the dendrites and the boutons. So part or all of the step may be a difference between two sets of recordings and not a transformation in the fly. These readings cannot separate the two.

**A lead on what the sets share.** The odors that are stronger in the megamat somata than in the control flies' somata tend to be the ones that rise from the dendrites to the boutons. Over the {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:n_odors ~ w}} shared odors the two differences follow each other at a Pearson r of {{E146/sets_odors_rms.csv:part=rate:r_sets_diff_vs_step_12 ~ .2f}} on the response rate and {{E146/sets_odors_rms.csv:part=mean:r_sets_diff_vs_step_12 ~ .2f}} on the mean response. The megamat somata and the boutons are one experimenter's recordings. On so few odors this is a lead and not a result.

**Beside: from the boutons to the claws the step is mostly a change of spread.** There the boutons' entry rescaled with no feature reads {{E146/beside_claws_features.csv:form=rescaled_alone;fly_set=all:left_out ~ .2f}} in the claw flies' one-fly units, where the boutons taken as the prediction read {{E146/beside_claws_features.csv:form=rescaled_alone;fly_set=all:no_step ~ .2f}}, and no feature does better. From the dendrites to the boutons the same rescaling gains nearly nothing. So the two steps differ in kind: one changes how widely the odors' strengths spread, and the other re-orders them.

**Realism.** No model was run or changed, so no realism band is read. The fitted weights enter no model and no reading, and no bouton region is given an identity. As anatomy they would be hard to defend: their rank correlation with the counts of boutons is {{E146/beside_counts_rho.csv:calyx=hemibrain-first:rho_in_sample_all ~ +.2f}} for the hemibrain, and {{E146/beside_counts_rho.csv:calyx=malecns-left:rho_in_sample_all ~ +.2f}} and {{E146/beside_counts_rho.csv:calyx=malecns-right:rho_in_sample_all ~ +.2f}} for the male CNS's two calyces.

**Limits.**

- **Two classes of form, on the recorded glomeruli.** A weighting is the same for every odor, and the features are few. A form in which glomeruli act on each other was not read. Neither were the {{diff:json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/timing_step1.json:size.n_input -- json:results/E135_antennal_lobe_dendrites/lock.json:len(glomeruli) ~ w}} input glomeruli that the dendrite recordings do not hold, nor which regions a bouton recording holds.
- **The method does not find a step as small as the model's own.** On the family's and the fitted model's saved dendrites and boutons the left-out readings are {{E146/beside_models.csv:group=family_held:left_out ~ .3f}} and {{E146/beside_models.csv:group=A1:left_out ~ .3f}}, against no-step yardsticks of {{E146/beside_models.csv:group=family_held:no_step ~ .3f}} and {{E146/beside_models.csv:group=A1:no_step ~ .3f}}. A step of that size is below what the check of power can find. The bound speaks for a step of the flies' size only.
- **The two sets that are compared are somata, not boutons.** They differ in more than the experimenter: the megamat flies are wild-type and the control flies are the control genotype of the APL experiments, the response criterion is set for each set, and the panels around the shared odors differ. One set is {{E146/sets.csv:comparison=megamat_in_control_units:n_scored ~ w}} flies. So the comparison gives a size that two sets can differ by, not the size that the dendrite and the bouton flies do differ by.
- **The flies are few.** The {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(recordings.pn_bouton.flies) ~ w}} bouton flies give halves of {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(recordings.pn_bouton.halves.fit) ~ w}} and {{json:results/E146_bouton_step_forms/lock.json:len(recordings.pn_bouton.halves.held) ~ w}}.
- **Strength by odor only.** Sharing between odors and breadth among units were not read.
- **Concentrations.** Megamat is at one concentration at both stages. The shared odors were given at the same concentrations in both sets of somata, and paa, euc and -aPine are not among them ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)).

**What follows.**

- **The lead of a step that re-orders the odors closes on the recorded glomeruli.** No form of the two classes makes the flies' step on odors that it was not fitted to.
- **Two decisions go to those who direct the project** ([pending](#pending)): how much the boutons weigh in the megamat stage error, and what a model is held to at a stage that was recorded in other flies, by another experimenter, than the stage before it.
- **Only the dendrites and the boutons recorded in the same flies would separate a step from a difference between sets.** Those would be new recordings.
- **The step from the ORNs to the PN dendrites is not taken up again** ([pending](#pending)). What was to reopen it was a form at this step.

### The weaker antennal-lobe normalization and the rise of the inhibition among KCs on the male CNS's two calyces {#male-calyces}

**The question was whether the fitted model's fall and its hold are one brain's.** The fitted model keeps the held-out panels only with the hemibrain's own matrix of the inhibition among KCs ([above](#kckc-rise)). All eight wirings of the family are that one brain's: its own and seven re-drawn from it. The experiment is the weaker antennal-lobe normalization and the rise of the inhibition among KCs on the male CNS's two calyces. It sets the fitted model's three changes on a second mapped brain, where none of them was chosen.

**It asked four things.**

- Whether the megamat stage error falls there as it does on the hemibrain.
- Whether the three changes hold there.
- Whether the rise of the inhibition among KCs is needed there, and at which dose.
- Where the family stands at each megamat stage on a second brain.

**The design.**

- **Two male calyces, four wirings each.** The male CNS's left calyx and its right calyx each have their own wiring, APL synapses and matrix of synapses between KCs. Each is read on {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:len(definitions.calyces.L.wirings) ~ w}} wirings: its own, and three with the claws permuted among the KCs of each type. A permuted wiring keeps the calyx's KCs, APL synapses and matrix, so it is not another fly.
- **The control is the hemibrain's own wiring under the claw rule of the male tables.** There each claw is placed on the bouton of its PN that gives it the most synapses. The family's eight wirings use another rule, so the control says whether the rule alone changes a class. It is "the hemibrain's control" below.
- **Nothing is fitted.** Each calyx starts from "the calyx's family": the family as the earlier work fitted it on that calyx to the APL16 recordings (the best family on the male CNS's two calyces). A setting is that family with factors set, and both response rates are held again at the operating points of the fit.
- **The settings add the fitted model's changes one at a time.** The first has the additive term of the PN normalization at {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:definitions.rung ~ ,.0f}} alone. The second adds the fit's scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons, "the bouton scale". Three more add the calyx's own matrix at {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:definitions.settings.D1.8.kckc ~ .1f}}, {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:definitions.rise ~ .2f}} and {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:definitions.settings.D5.8.kckc ~ .2f}} times its fitted scale: "the lower dose", the fitted model's factor and "the higher dose". The setting at the fitted model's factor is "the three changes".
- **One more setting on the left calyx.** Its family's scale of the matrix is {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:fits.L.params.wKCKC_scale ~ .1f}}, against {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:fits.R.params.wKCKC_scale ~ .1f}} on the right calyx and {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:definitions.hemibrain_real_kckc ~ .1f}} on the hemibrain's own wiring as the family has it. So the left calyx's matrix is also set at {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:definitions.dabs_scale ~ .1f}}, the scale that the fitted model's factor reaches on that wiring of the hemibrain: "the matrix at the hemibrain's scale".
- **The reads.** There are {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=reads;statistic=n:value ~ .0f}}, each of one setting on one wiring with one panel's input: megamat's four stages, or the somata of validation2, of the mixtures or of the APL16 panel. The calyx's family, the bouton scale and the three doses were read on all four wirings of a male calyx. The normalization alone, the matrix at the hemibrain's scale and every setting of the control were read on one wiring.
- **The lower and the higher dose were read on one wiring first.** They were read on a calyx's permuted wirings only because the three changes held on neither male calyx, as planned.
- **The classes are the fit's** ([above](#stage-fit)), with the price on the APL effect of the settings along the normalization ([above](#normalization-ladder)). A setting is read against its own calyx's family on the same wirings, megamat against the held-out half of the flies. The bar is twice the standard error over the calyx's wirings, or the spread where one wiring is read. The spreads are the hemibrain family's over its eight wirings, and a setting has no class if a response rate cannot be held.
- **Every class was read again with the flies resampled** {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/e143_intervals.json:n_boot ~ ,.0f}} times, as in the megamat stage error through the flies' sampling ([above](#fly-sampling)). That reading says how firm a class is, and it gates nothing.

The family at megamat's stages on each calyx, on strength by odor in one-fly units; lower is better. But for the row against all the flies, values are against the held-out half of each stage's flies, so they are compared with the values of the fit ([above](#stage-fit)) and not with the stage table. "Four wirings" is the mean over a male calyx's own wiring and its three permuted ones. The last row sets the three changes on each calyx.

| | the hemibrain {span=2} | the male CNS's left calyx {span=2} | the male CNS's right calyx {span=2} |

**On the male CNS the family is further from the flies from the boutons on.** On its own wiring each male calyx's family is above the highest of the hemibrain's eight wirings at the boutons, at the claws and at the somata, and inside their range at the dendrites. The right calyx's boutons are furthest out. Against all the flies, as the stage table is read, both male families' megamat stage errors are above the hemibrain's eight wirings as well.

**This is the first reading of the family at megamat's stages on a second brain, and it is a held-out one.** The male families were fitted to the APL16 recordings and to no megamat stage, as the hemibrain's family was. So a model built on the male CNS starts further from the flies after the dendrites than one built on the hemibrain.

**The boutons follow what the male counts of boutons predicted.** With each glomerulus counted by its boutons in the male CNS, the recorded dendrites read worse against the bouton flies than with each counted once, and worst with the right calyx's counts ([above](#dendrite-bouton-step)). The families' boutons stand in that order.

**At the somata the male families' distance is mostly aa's.** Without aa's entry the left family's somata read {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=without_aa:value ~ .3f}} in place of {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=with_aa:value ~ .3f}}, and the right family's {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_family;statistic=without_aa:value ~ .3f}} in place of {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_family;statistic=with_aa:value ~ .3f}}. The hemibrain's control reads {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=H_family;statistic=without_aa:value ~ .3f}} in place of {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=H_family;statistic=with_aa:value ~ .3f}}. So with aa left out the male families' somata are nearer the flies than the hemibrain's. A single odor's entry is a lead ([above](#claw-soma-aa)).

**The outcome: megamat falls on every calyx, and the three changes hold on neither male calyx.** With the three changes the megamat stage error falls on the left calyx, on the right calyx and on the hemibrain's control, each by more than its bar. The fall is made at the dendrites, the boutons and the claws, as on the hemibrain's eight wirings. The class is strength at another view's cost on the left calyx, and megamat only on the right calyx and on the hemibrain's control.

The three changes on the hemibrain's control, and the settings read on four wirings of each male calyx: each against its own calyx's family on the same wirings, on strength by odor in one-fly units. A negative change is nearer the flies. Megamat is read against the held-out half of the flies, and a held-out panel's cell is the change of its KC somata. A change is the mean over the wirings read, set against the hemibrain family's spread in the first row.

A fall of the megamat stage error counts beyond a bar of {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;stage=stage;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}}, or of {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_A1;stage=stage;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}} and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_D5.8;stage=stage;statistic=bar:value ~ .3f}} for the three changes and the higher dose on the left calyx. A held-out panel fails on its strength error, as shown, or on its error over all three views. The rise of the pattern part of the APL-effect loss is from the calyx's family, the largest of its three readings: against all the recordings and against each half of them. The last column is the share of the draws of the flies that give the class as scored.

**Of the {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:len(classes) ~ w}} settings read on three calyces, {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:len(calyces.R.settings_holding) ~ w}} holds: the higher dose on the right calyx.** Every setting on every calyx lowers the megamat stage error by more than its bar. The bouton scale without the rise holds on no calyx, so the need for the rise is not the hemibrain's alone.

**The settings read on one wiring add no hold.** On the hemibrain's control the other four settings are megamat only, as the three changes are. On each male calyx's own wiring the normalization alone is one stage at another's cost: megamat's somata move away from the flies.

**The left calyx fails at the megamat somata's other views.** From the three changes up, every held-out panel is nearer the flies there than under the left family. But the megamat somata's error over all three views rises by more than its spread. Along the dose their strength error alone falls, from {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_B;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} with the bouton scale to {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_D5.8;stage=kc_soma;panel=megamat;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} at the higher dose, while their error over all views stays above the family's. That class is firm through the flies' sampling (the last column).

**The right calyx fails at the APL16 panel's somata with APL on,** the reading that set the lower end of the doses on the hemibrain ([above](#kckc-rise)). With the three changes they are worse than their spread. At the higher dose they come just inside it, and that is the one hold.

**That hold is a near-tie.** It is read on {{E144/e143_class_shares.csv:arm=R_D5.8:share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws of the flies. The right calyx's changes sit at the spread of the APL16 panel's somata, so all of its classes turn on which flies are drawn. No dose or calyx becomes a base from such a class.

**On the hemibrain's own wiring no setting holds, under either claw rule.** That wiring under the family's own claw rule gives the same classes as the control. So the fitted model's hold was a hold of the mean over its four held-out wirings. With the flies resampled that hold is read on {{E143_megamat_fly_sampling/class_shares.csv:table=E136_stage_population_fit;arm=A1:share_scored ~ .2f}} of the draws ([above](#fly-sampling)).

**The claw rule changes no class, and it moves one stage.** With the fit's bouton scale the boutons' error differs by {{max:E144/writeup_values.csv:stage=pn_bouton;statistic=diff_first;variant!=H_family;variant!=H_F:value ~ +.3f}} to {{min:E144/writeup_values.csv:stage=pn_bouton;statistic=diff_first;variant!=H_family;variant!=H_F:value ~ +.3f}} over the four settings: lower under the male tables' rule, by more than the spread of {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:spreads.pn_bouton ~ .3f}}. So each male calyx is read against its own family, as planned.

The lower and the higher dose on a male calyx's own wiring alone, as first read, and on its four wirings. A change is of the strength error of the APL16 panel's KC somata with APL on, from the calyx's family. Negative is nearer the flies, and the hemibrain family's spread is {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:spreads.apl16/apl16/on ~ .3f}}.

| | | class {span=2} | APL16 panel's KC somata with APL on, change {span=2} |
| calyx | dose | its own wiring alone | four wirings | its own wiring alone | four wirings |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| the male CNS's right calyx | the higher dose | {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/step1_score/classes.json:classes.R_D5.8.cls}} | {{E144/e143_class_shares.csv:arm=R_D5.8:cls_scored}} | {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/step1_score/classes.json:classes.R_D5.8.panels.apl16/apl16/on.change ~ +.3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_D5.8;panel=apl16;condition=on;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} |

**One wiring is a weak guide to a calyx's four.** On the right calyx the higher dose is megamat only on its own wiring and holds on four. On the left calyx no class changed.

**On the left calyx the highest scales cannot be held.** On its own wiring the higher dose and the matrix at the hemibrain's scale drive APL's scale to the top of its range, {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_D5.8;condition=real;statistic=apl_factor:value ~ .0f}} times its start. The response rate with APL on still ends at {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_D5.8;condition=real;statistic=rate_on:value ~ .4f}} and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_Dabs;condition=real;statistic=rate_on:value ~ .4f}}, against the flies' {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:definitions.operating_points.megamat ~ .4f}}, so both settings have no class. On the three permuted wirings, where the rates are held, the higher dose reads as the three changes do (the row marked as a description).

Validation2's KC somata along the settings: the change of the strength error from the calyx's family; negative is nearer the flies. The hemibrain family's spread there is {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:spreads.validation2/validation2/on ~ .3f}}.

**The ladder has the hemibrain's shape on every calyx, and it is steeper on the male CNS.** The bouton scale without the rise moves validation2's somata away from the flies, and each step of the rise brings them back. On the left calyx's own wiring the matrix at the hemibrain's scale changes them by {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_Dabs;panel=validation2;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}, in a setting that has no class.

With the flies resampled: the share of the {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/e143_intervals.json:n_boot ~ ,.0f}} draws on which each statement is true.

| statistic | the hemibrain's control | the male CNS's left calyx | the male CNS's right calyx |
|---|---|---|---|
| the megamat stage error falls with the three changes | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=H_derived;stage=a1_megamat_falls;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_derived;stage=a1_megamat_falls;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_derived;stage=a1_megamat_falls;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} |
| the three changes hold | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=H_derived;stage=a1_holds;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_derived;stage=a1_holds;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_derived;stage=a1_holds;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} |
| the bouton scale holds without the rise | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=H_derived;stage=b_holds;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_derived;stage=b_holds;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_derived;stage=b_holds;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} |
| the rise is needed: the bouton scale does not hold, and a dose does | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=H_derived;stage=rise_needed;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_derived;stage=rise_needed;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_derived;stage=rise_needed;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} |

**The verdict stands on most draws.** A fall on both male calyces with a hold on neither is read on {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=outcome;stage=b;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}} of the draws, and a hold of the three changes on one male calyx alone on {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=outcome;stage=c;statistic=share_true:value ~ .3f}}. The left calyx's classes are firm, and the right calyx's are not.

**The shares are less exact here than on the hemibrain.** For them each model is read once against all the flies. At a megamat stage error that reading differs from the score's own by up to {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_shortcut;statistic=max_abs_diff:value ~ .3f}} on the left calyx and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_shortcut;statistic=max_abs_diff:value ~ .3f}} on the right, beyond the tolerance of {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/e143_intervals.json:shortcut_check.tol ~ .2f}} set beforehand. On the hemibrain's control it differs by {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=H_shortcut;statistic=max_abs_diff:value ~ .3f}}.

**The male matrices keep what the shuffle took from the hemibrain's.** The matrix shuffled within pairs of KC types did not hold in the matrix's place ([above](#kckc-rise)). The shuffle removes nearly all of the matrix's reciprocity and of its preference for pairs of KCs that share an input glomerulus, and it evens out the KCs' summed input. Both male matrices keep all three.

**Their reciprocity and their preference are near the hemibrain's or above it.** The share of entries whose reverse entry is also there is {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=L:reciprocal_share ~ .2f}} and {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=R:reciprocal_share ~ .2f}}, against the hemibrain's {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=hemibrain:reciprocal_share ~ .2f}} and the shuffle's {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=hemibrain_SH:reciprocal_share ~ .2f}}. The weight between KCs that share an input glomerulus is {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=L:shared_glomerulus_ratio ~ .2f}} and {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=R:shared_glomerulus_ratio ~ .2f}} times what the number of such pairs gives, against {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=hemibrain:shared_glomerulus_ratio ~ .2f}} and {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=hemibrain_SH:shared_glomerulus_ratio ~ .2f}}.

**They differ in density and in the mix of KC types.** A KC has {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=L:inputs_per_kc ~ .1f}} inputs from other KCs in the left calyx and {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=R:inputs_per_kc ~ .1f}} in the right, against {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=hemibrain:inputs_per_kc ~ .1f}} in the hemibrain. Of the weight, {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=L:within_type_weight_share ~ .2f}} and {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=R:within_type_weight_share ~ .2f}} lies between KCs of one type, against {{E144/step0_matrices.csv:matrix=hemibrain:within_type_weight_share ~ .2f}}. So what the hemibrain's matrix has and the male ones lack is none of the three features that the shuffle removes.

**Between the brains the difference is one of degree and of dose.** On the right calyx the calyx's own matrix keeps the held-out panels at the higher dose, by a near-tie. On the hemibrain's own wiring no dose keeps them. So that only the hemibrain's matrix holds is a statement about the forms tried on the hemibrain's wirings, and not about brains.

The APL effect on the APL16 panel for the male calyces, a test on recordings that no fit of the stages saw: the mean over a calyx's four wirings, read through the calibrated readout against all the recordings ([how the statistics are defined](#fn-apl-effect)). Each calyx's family was fitted to these recordings, so its row is a fit. Lower is better for the pattern part and its parts.

| model | pattern part | amplitude part /<br>shape part | the model's spread of the<br>per-pair APL effect as a<br>multiple of the flies' | ρ on the per-pair<br>APL effect | Pearson r on the<br>per-pair APL effect | mean APL effect |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| the left calyx's family | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=price_z8_pattern:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=price_z8_amplitude:value ~ .3f}} / {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=price_z8_shape:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=price_z8_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=price_z8_rho:value ~ .2f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=price_z8_pearson:value ~ .2f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=price_mean_effect:value ~ +.3f}} |

| the right calyx with the three changes | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=price_z8_pattern:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=price_z8_amplitude:value ~ .3f}} / {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=price_z8_shape:value ~ .3f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=price_z8_sd_ratio:value ~ .2f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=price_z8_rho:value ~ .2f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=price_z8_pearson:value ~ .2f}} | {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=price_mean_effect:value ~ +.3f}} |

**Every setting pays a price on the APL effect, on every calyx** (the class table's column). With the three changes the pattern part rises in its shape part, and both correlations fall. On the male calyces the price is smaller than on the hemibrain's control, and the male families start further from the flies than the hemibrain's. These rows describe a held-out test, and no setting is chosen by them.

**Realism.** On the APL16 input no wiring of any setting on any calyx leaves a realism band, and none is flagged. Both response rates are held in every read but the two on the left calyx's own wiring.

- **The band that the holds do not fix.** With the three changes the response rate ρ with APL on is {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_A1;statistic=rate_rho_on_min:value ~ .2f}} on the left calyx's lowest wiring and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;statistic=rate_rho_on_min:value ~ .2f}} on the right's, against the band's {{const:kcq/apl_effect.py:RATE_RHO_MIN ~ .2f}}. At the higher dose the right calyx's lowest wiring has {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_D5.8;statistic=rate_rho_on_min:value ~ .2f}}.
- **The rise takes the scale of the inhibition among KCs far above the fitted one.** On the left calyx it goes from the family's {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:fits.L.params.wKCKC_scale ~ .1f}} to {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_A1;panel=megamat;statistic=wKCKC_scale_max:value ~ .1f}} with the three changes and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_D5.8;panel=megamat;statistic=wKCKC_scale_max:value ~ .1f}} at the higher dose. On the right calyx the one setting that holds has it at {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_D5.8;panel=megamat;statistic=wKCKC_scale_max:value ~ .1f}}, against the family's {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/E144_lock.json:fits.R.params.wKCKC_scale ~ .1f}}, for a term with no named carrier.
- **The largest spike counts rise with it.** With APL on, a KC's largest count on megamat goes from {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;panel=megamat;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} under the left calyx's family to {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_A1;panel=megamat;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} with the three changes, and from {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_family;panel=megamat;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} under the right calyx's family to {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_D5.8;panel=megamat;statistic=max_count_on_max:value ~ .0f}} at the higher dose.
- **Where APL acts.** On megamat the left and the right calyx's families take {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;panel=megamat;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_family;panel=megamat;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} of the drive that APL removes from the claws at the PN boutons, where the male CNS puts {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_family;statistic=anatomical_bouton_share:value ~ .2f}} and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_family;statistic=anatomical_bouton_share:value ~ .2f}} of APL's calyx synapses. With the three changes the shares are {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=L_A1;panel=megamat;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}} and {{E144/writeup_values.csv:variant=R_A1;panel=megamat;statistic=bouton_share_mean:value ~ .2f}}.
- **The cost of the gains is the rise's.** The bands hold. What would be hard to defend is the scale of an inhibition with no carrier, the higher spike counts and the price on the APL effect.

**Limits.**

- **The flies are few after the dendrites.** Megamat's claws and somata are read against two flies each, and they do not order forms of the calyx ([above](#fly-sampling)). The left calyx's class rests on the megamat somata.
- **The resampled reading is a description,** and its shares are less exact on the male calyces than on the hemibrain.
- **The spreads and the bars are the hemibrain family's.** The male calyces' own standard deviations of the megamat stage error over their four wirings are {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:calyces.L.own_sd_family.stage ~ .3f}} and {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:calyces.R.own_sd_family.stage ~ .3f}}, against {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:spreads.stage ~ .3f}}.
- **The permuted wirings are not other flies.** The control, the normalization alone and the matrix at the hemibrain's scale were read on one wiring each.
- **The male families are fits to the APL16 recordings.** Nothing was refitted, so their standing at megamat's stages is a held-out reading. No fit of the megamat stages has been made on the male CNS.
- **The same held-out panels judged {{json:results/E144_a1_malecns_calyces/classes.json:len(classes) ~ w}} more settings,** and validation2 has served once as a test set ([caveats](#caveats)). Nothing here was chosen from that regression.
- **Concentrations.** Every number of the APL16 panel involves paa, euc and -aPine ([footnote](#fn-concentrations)).

**What follows.**

- **The fitted model's fall on megamat is not one brain's.** It repeats on both male calyces and on the hemibrain's control, on every draw of the flies.
- **Its hold has not been found outside the mean of the hemibrain's four held-out wirings:** not on either male calyx, and not on the hemibrain's own wiring read alone. The fits since made on the hemibrain's wirings, on the other wirings and against the other halves of the flies, do not find it either ([below](#stage-fit-again)).
- **The one hold, on the right calyx at the higher dose, is a near-tie** and becomes no base. It would have to be confirmed on other held-out flies or wirings.
- **What the hemibrain's matrix has that the male ones lack is a lead** ([pending](#pending)). It would be read in density and in the mix of KC types, since the male matrices keep what the shuffle removes.
- **A model on the male CNS starts further from the flies after the dendrites:** at the boutons on both calyces, and at aa's entry at the somata.

### The fit of all megamat stages made again on the other wirings and the other halves of the flies {#stage-fit-again}

**The question was whether the fitted model's fall, its hold and its fitted factors are those of one split.** The fitted model is one fit. It was made on one half of the family's wirings against one half of each stage's flies, and read on the other wirings against the other flies ([above](#stage-fit)). With the flies resampled its hold is a near-tie ([above](#fly-sampling)), and it was found on no single wiring and on neither male calyx ([above](#male-calyces)). The experiment is the fit of all megamat stages made again on the other wirings and the other halves of the flies.

**The design.**

- **One more fit for each other combination** of a half of the wirings with a half of each stage's flies, {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/classes.json:len(experiment.cells_scored) ~ w}} in all. "Both exchanged" is fitted on the other wirings against the other flies. "The other flies" keeps the first fit's wirings, and "the other wirings" keeps its flies. Each is read on the wirings and against the flies that it did not use, and the unrefitted family is read there too.
- **Nothing else is changed.** The loss, the searched factors and their ranges, the response rates that are held and the start at the family's own point are the first fit's ([above](#stage-fit)). The searched factors are the additive term and the scale of the PN normalization, the scale of APL's inhibition of the PN boutons ("the bouton scale") and the scale of the inhibition among KCs.
- **A factor is "determined"** if returning it alone to the family's value raises the fit's loss by {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:fit.not_determined ~ .3f}} or more. A factor that is not determined is returned to the family's value before the fit is read.
- **The classes are the fit's** ([above](#stage-fit)), with the price on the APL effect of the settings along the normalization ([above](#normalization-ladder)). Each fit is classed against the unrefitted family where it is read. A fit that ends at a bound of its search has no class.
- **Statements over the new fits were fixed beforehand.**
  - "The fall repeats" if the megamat stage error falls in every new fit by more than its bar and by more than the control fit's fall.
  - "The hold repeats" if the fit with both exchanged holds, and one of the other new fits with it.
  - "The megamat stages fix a factor" if it comes back in every new fit: the additive term between {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:repeat.inhadd.lo ~ .0f}} and {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:repeat.inhadd.hi ~ .0f}}, the bouton scale determined and between {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:repeat.aplpn.lo ~ .3f}} and {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:repeat.aplpn.hi ~ .2f}} times the family's, the scale of the inhibition among KCs determined and at {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:repeat.kckc.lo ~ .1f}} times the family's or more.
- **Every class was read again with the flies resampled** {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/E145_lock.json:resampled.n ~ .0f}} times, as in the megamat stage error through the flies' sampling ([above](#fly-sampling)). That reading gates nothing.

The fits where each is read, on strength by odor in one-fly units; lower is better, and a negative change is nearer the flies. A value is the mean over the {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/classes.json:len(cells.X.read_wirings) ~ w}} wirings that the fit did not use, megamat against the half of each stage's flies that it did not see. A change is from the unrefitted family read the same way, set against the family's spread over its eight wirings in the first row. A fall counts beyond the fit's bar and beyond the control fit's fall of {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/classes.json:control_fall ~ .3f}}. The last column is the share of the draws of the flies that give the class as scored.

**The outcome: the fall repeats in every fit, and the hold in none.** Each new fit lowers the megamat stage error where it is read, by more than its bar and by more than the control fit's fall. None of them holds. One ends at a bound of its search and has no class, one lowers megamat at the claws' cost, and one at the cost of the somata's other views. So the gain before the calyx is not one split's, and the fitted model's hold is.

**The new fits gain more at the PN dendrites than the first fit, and the stages after them pay.** The dendrites' strength error changes by {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=X;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=read;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} with both exchanged, {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=F;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=read;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} against the other flies and {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=W;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=read;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}} on the other wirings, against the first fit's {{E145/writeup_values.csv:arm=A1;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=read;statistic=change:value ~ +.3f}}. With both exchanged and against the other flies the claws move away from the flies by more than their spread, where the first fit's came nearer. On the other wirings the claws come nearer, and the somata's error over all three views rises by more than its spread. A stage's share of a fall is a lead.

The KC somata of the held-out panels on strength by odor: each fit's change from the unrefitted family, in one-fly units; negative is nearer the flies. A change is the mean over the wirings that the fit did not use, each held at its response rates for that panel, against all the panel's flies. It is set against the family's spread in the first row. The kiwi mixtures are read as uploaded and stand beside the test ([caveats](#caveats)).

**Every new fit moves a held-out panel away from the flies.** The APL16 panel's somata with APL on are worse than their spread in every new fit. validation2's are worse with both exchanged and against the other flies, and the control mixtures' on the other wirings. With both exchanged the APL16 panel's somata with APL off are worse as well, on their error over all three views: {{json:results/E145_stage_fit_replication/classes.json:cells.X.cls.guard.apl16/apl16/off.change ~ +.3f}} against a spread of {{E136/measure_strength/writeup_values.csv:arm=family;stage=kc_soma;panel=apl16/apl16/off;condition=on;half=held;statistic=spread_all_views:value ~ .3f}}. The first fit moved none of these panels by more than its spread.

**Beside the test, the kiwi mixtures move away as well.** Read as uploaded, their somata are further from the flies than the family's spread in every new fit, where the first fit's stayed inside it. That reading gates nothing ([caveats](#caveats)).

The fitted factors of the fits. The additive term is in the model's units, in which the family's is {{E139/writeup_values.csv:arm=pn_port_family;stage=pn_dendrite;panel=megamat;condition=on;half=held;statistic=pn_inhadd:value ~ .1f}}. The scales are multiples of the family's. "When returned" is the rise of the fit's loss, on the fitted wirings against the fitted half of the flies, when that factor alone is returned to the family's value. The loss holds the megamat stage error and the penalty for response rates that cannot be held ([above](#stage-fit)). The last column is the ρ, over a fit's evaluations, between their megamat stage errors against the fitted half of the flies and against the other half; {{json:results/E134_population_baseline/lock.json:rules.gate.B_reading.min_consistency}} is asked of a measure.
